### [1] [Topological Transitions of Evanescent Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07038)
*Ignacio Pavez,Tom Sheppard,Yoonseok Hwang,Hannah M. Price,Frank Schindler*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文研究一维紧束缚模型中能隙内倏逝波的复波矢色散，发现由无能隙时间反演对称性保护的新型"隙内奇异点"(EP)，并证明其由能隙内每个能量所携带的Z_2指标诊断，EP为该指标畴壁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上能隙内的倏逝/衰减模式被视为无关紧要，且奇异点通常只在带边出现。作者希望系统刻画虽底层哈密顿量为厄米、但复波矢导致非厄米色散这一情形下能隙内的谱结构，澄清是否存在带边以外的奇异点及其拓扑意义。

Method: 采用一维转移矩阵形式，分析复波矢分支的色散关系；结合BdG幺正变换，在无能隙时间反演对称性下研究隙内EP对的成核；定义能隙内每个能量对应的Z_2指标，识别其为EP作为畴壁；并用连续模型、紧束缚模型以及各向异性光子晶体进行验证。

Result: 除带边的标准EP外，能隙内部还存在由无能隙时间反演对称性保护的另一类EP。调节模型参数可使纯虚动量分支交叉，介导EP对的成核，从而驱动拓扑相变。该谱结构由能隙内每个能量的Z_2指标诊断，隙内EP即为其畴壁。束缚态尾部的形态取决于能量位置：位于带边与最近EP之间时尾为纯指数衰减，位于隙内EP对之间时则变为振荡，且波矢不同于带边动量。

Conclusion: 能隙内倏逝波具有受对称性保护的非厄米奇异点谱结构，可由Z_2拓扑指标统一描述；这为在厄米晶格及光子晶体中识别和操控隙内EP及束缚态空间形态提供了判据。

Abstract: We study the band structure of evanescent waves, that is, the decaying or growing solutions that live within the gaps of tight-binding Hamiltonians. Although the underlying Hamiltonian is Hermitian, these modes carry complex wavevectors and therefore exhibit a non-Hermitian dispersion. Using the transfer matrix formalism in one dimension, we show that, in addition to the standard exceptional points that appear at the band edges, a different type of exceptional point can arise inside the gap, protected by spinless time-reversal symmetry. Tuning model parameters drives a topological transition where such in-gap exceptional points are nucleated in pairs, mediated by a crossing between purely imaginary momentum branches. We show that this spectral structure is diagnosed by a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ index assigned to every energy inside the gap, with the in-gap exceptional points arising as its domain walls. A generic bound state in the system has a tail that is purely exponential when its energy lies between a band edge and the nearest exceptional point, but becomes oscillatory - with a wavevector differing from the band-edge momentum - when its energy lies between the pair of in-gap exceptional points. We illustrate these results using continuum and tight-binding models, as well as an anisotropic photonic crystal.

</details>


### [2] [Parity-Selective Quantum Interference in Unconventional Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07330)
*Yiyuan Chen,Xiao-Bin Qiang,Hai-Zhou Lu,Congjun Wu,X. C. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 量子干涉可作为区分非常规磁性态自旋劈裂宇称的对称性分辨探针：偶宇称磁体表现为可被自旋轨道耦合抑制的弱局域化，奇宇称磁体则具有可被自旋轨道耦合释放的"隐藏"弱反局域化，并给出对应的磁电导公式以供实验检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非相对论性自旋劈裂已成为非常规磁性态的标志性特征，但缺乏能够区分其宇称（偶/奇）的实用探测手段；本文旨在提出一种基于量子干涉的对称性分辨诊断方法。

Method: 从弱（反）局域化理论出发，利用自旋群刻画时间反演对称的散射环路，分析偶宇称与奇宇称磁体中散射环路的对称性差异，并推导相应的磁电导修正公式。

Result: 偶宇称磁体可存在由自旋群描述的时间反演对称散射环路，产生可被自旋轨道耦合抑制的弱局域化；奇宇称磁体则具有隐藏的弱反局域化，可在自旋轨道耦合作用下显现。同时给出可用于拟合实验的磁电导公式。

Conclusion: 将弱（反）局域化理论推广至非常规磁体，并提供了一种检验非常规自旋劈裂磁性态的实用诊断手段。

Abstract: Nonrelativistic spin splitting has emerged as a defining feature of unconventional magnetic states. Here we show that quantum interference offers a symmetry-resolved probe for the parity of such spin splitting, as even- and odd-parity magnets exhibit qualitatively different weak (anti-)localization behaviors. Even-parity magnets can host time-reversal symmetric scattering loops depicted by the spin group, giving rise to a weak localization that can be suppressed by spin-orbit coupling. In contrast, odd-parity magnets have a hidden weak antilocalization that can be released by spinorbit coupling. We further derive the corresponding magnetoconductivity formulas to facilitate experimental explorations. Our results not only advance the weak (anti-)localization theory to unconventional magnets but also suggest a practical diagnostic of unconventional spin-split magnetic states.

</details>


### [3] [Prediction of antimagnon-polarons with intrinsic momentum-space level attraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07434)
*Chongzhou Wang,Zhengda Jing,Weichao Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文预测在连续磁弹介质中存在"反磁振子-极化子"(antimagnon-polaron)，其本征呈现动量空间能级吸引，并可实现直流泵浦、频率连续可调的单频声子激光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁振子与声子/光子形成的杂化准粒子通常表现为动量空间的能级排斥；能级吸引此前仅限于由耗散浴介导的零维系统，需要反相通道，缺乏在连续介质中本征实现吸引的机制。

Method: 理论预测自旋轨道矩(SOT)稳定的反磁振子具有相反手性，从而无需外部环境即可直接反转磁弹耦合；结合微磁学模拟验证增益与阻尼平衡所驱动的集体不稳定性。

Result: 反磁振子-极化子在连续磁弹介质中本征展现动量空间能级吸引，并产生特征性的0/π相位锁定的同时增长；微磁学模拟证实SOT调控的增益-阻尼平衡引发集体不稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作提出无需外接环境的内禀能级吸引机制，并据此实现连续可调、直流泵浦的单频声子激光，为相干声发射开辟新途径。

Abstract: Propagating hybrid quasiparticles formed by magnons and phonons (magnon-polarons) or photons (magnon-polaritons) typically exhibit level repulsion in momentum space. Level attraction, by contrast, requires an anti-phase channel and has so far been restricted to zero-dimensional systems mediated by dissipative baths. Here we predict the antimagnon-polaron in a continuous magnetoelastic medium, which inherently exhibits momentum-space level attraction. The spin-orbit-torque (SOT)-stabilized antimagnon precesses with opposite handedness, directly reversing the magnetoelastic coupling without requiring an external environment. This intrinsic attraction yields a distinctive 0/π phase-locked simultaneous growth. Micromagnetic simulations confirm this picture, revealing that the SOT-controlled balance between gain and damping drives a collective instability. This ultimately enables a continuously tunable, dc-pumped, single-frequency phonon laser, establishing a novel route for coherent acoustic emission.

</details>


### [4] [Supermoiré Formation and Robust Topological Edge State in Helical Trilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07435)
*Hong Li,Huandong Chen,Seongyeon Youn,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Trithep Devakul,Kazuhiro Fujita,Abhay Narayan Pasupathy*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用STM/STS在~1.7°扭转的螺旋三层石墨烯中直接观测到超莫尔畴结构，畴内存在被~40 meV大能隙分隔的平带，畴壁呈现谷保护的拓扑边缘态，并通过子晶格极化反转揭示畴界处的拓扑变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两晶格常数近乎匹配的材料会形成由非公度畴壁分隔的公度畴；该图像可推广到莫尔超晶格，即"超莫尔"结构。螺旋三层石墨烯（HTG）被理论预言其公度畴为不同的拓扑绝缘体、畴壁为导电网络，但缺乏原子尺度的实验验证。

Method: 采用扫描隧道显微镜与扫描隧道谱（STM/STS）对扭转角约1.7°的HTG进行实空间成像与局域谱学测量，并结合理论计算对比。

Result: 获得原子尺度的超莫尔畴弛豫证据；每个畴内平带与远带之间具有约40 meV的大能隙；畴壁沿长度方向表现出谱学上均匀的边缘态，与谷保护拓扑边缘模的计算高度吻合；通过成像子晶格极化反转，直接观测到跨畴界的拓扑性质改变。

Conclusion: 实验证实HTG中存在超莫尔畴及其拓扑畴壁网络，平带拓扑性与石墨烯子晶格上的波函数局域密切相关，为基于超莫尔结构的可调控拓扑相与一维边缘态器件提供平台。

Abstract: When two materials with nearly matched lattice constants meet, they form commensurate domains separated by incommensurate domain walls that absorb the mismatch. Recently, this picture has been extended to moiré lattices: when two moiré superlattices have nearly equal periodicities, they can form a "supermoiré" consisting of commensurate moiré domains separated by domain walls. Helical trilayer graphene (HTG) is a particularly interesting example: theory predicts that its commensurate domains are distinct topological insulators, separated by a network of conducting domain walls. Here, we use scanning tunneling microscopy and spectroscopy to visualize supermoiré domains and their electronic properties in HTG at a twist angle of $\sim1.7^\circ$. We find atomic-scale evidence of relaxation into supermoiré domains, each hosting flat bands separated from remote bands by large gaps of $\sim40$ meV. Domain walls exhibit spectrally uniform edge states along their length, in excellent agreement with calculations for valley-protected topological edge modes. The topological character of the flat bands is intimately tied to wavefunction localization on a graphene sublattice, and we reveal the topological change across a domain boundary by imaging the reversal of sublattice polarization.

</details>


### [5] [Probing Non-Abelian Topology through Quench Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07456)
*Zhenming Zhang,Tianyu Li,Wei Yi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种非阿贝尔拓扑绝缘体的淬火方案，通过动力学 skyrmion 与带隙分辨的陈数重构静态哈密顿量的完整非阿贝尔拓扑及边界态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有淬火动力学探测主要限于阿贝尔能带拓扑或能带节点的欧拉类拓扑，多带系统中非阿贝尔拓扑绝缘体的淬火动力学尚不清楚，其本征态交织几何需用非对易拓扑荷描述。

Method: 设计针对非阿贝尔拓扑绝缘体的淬火协议；发展能带投影方案，从淬火过程中(准动量-时间)涌现流形上的动力学 skyrmion 提取带隙分辨拓扑，并用一系带隙分辨的动力学陈数表征。

Result: 由带隙分辨动力学陈数可重构淬火后哈密顿量的完整非阿贝尔拓扑，包括全局非阿贝尔拓扑荷以及开边界条件下的边界态模式。

Conclusion: 该方案为多带系统中的非阿贝尔拓扑提供动力学探测途径，并可在光子或冷原子体系中实现。

Abstract: Quench dynamics offer valuable insights to topological matter, wherein the emergent topological phenomena serve as a dynamic probe to the topology of static Hamiltonians. While such a practice has been widely applied to detect the Abelian band topology or the Euler-class topology of band nodes, little is known regarding the quench dynamics of non-Abelian topological insulators in multiband systems, where the intertwining geometry of all eigenstates necessitates the description of noncommutative topological charges. Here we propose a quench protocol for non-Abelian topological insulators, so that the underlying topology can be systematically probed using dynamic signatures. Specifically, we develop a band-projection scheme to extract the gap-resolved topology from dynamic skyrmions in the emergent quasimomentum--time manifolds of the quench processes. These skyrmions are characterized by a series of gap-resolved dynamic Chern numbers, which enable us to reconstruct the full non-Abelian topology of the post-quench Hamiltonian, including the global non-Abelian topological charge and edge-state patterns under the open-boundary condition. Our proposal offers dynamical access to non-Abelian topology in multiband systems, and can be readily implemented using photons or cold atoms.

</details>


### [6] [Ultrafast Moiré Exciton Dynamics Revealed by Two-Dimensional Coherent Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07539)
*Deran Zhang,Zhexue Chen,Dongxue Chen,Tengfei Yan,Jianhua Jiang,Hui Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用二维相干光谱直接分辨hBN封装WS₂/WSe₂异质结中莫尔限域层内WS₂ A激子的7个离散能级（限域深度57.02 meV），并观测到两阶段热化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔超晶格可将激子限域到离散能级，但非均匀展宽掩盖了其内部弛豫路径，缺乏态分辨的实验手段来揭示激子热化动力学。

Method: 采用二维相干光谱（2DCS）在hBN封装的WS₂/WSe₂范德华异质结中分辨莫尔限域层内WS₂ A激子的分立能级，并在皮秒延迟尺度上追踪各能级布居演化，结合Kullback–Leibler散度分析布居分布。

Result: 分辨出7个离散能级，对应限域深度57.02 meV；观察到两阶段热化：1.2 ps内有效激子温度由171.8 K降至99.3 K（能级组内快速布居重分布），随后经激子–环境相互作用缓慢回升趋向准平衡；KL散度证实布居由非平衡向玻尔兹曼分布逐步演化。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了对莫尔激子动力学的态分辨探测能力，为研究莫尔量子材料中的非平衡多体现象提供了基础。

Abstract: Moiré superlattices of van der Waals heterostructures confine excitons into discrete energy levels in finite-sized samples, yet their internal relaxation pathways remain obscured by inhomogeneous broadening. Here, using two-dimensional coherent spectroscopy (2DCS), we directly resolve seven discrete energy levels of the moiré-confined intralayer $\mathrm{WS}_{2}$ A exciton in an hBN-encapsulated $\mathrm{WS}_{2}/\mathrm{WSe}_{2}$ heterostructure, corresponding to a confinement depth of 57.02 meV. By tracking the population of these levels over picosecond delays, we uncover a two-stage thermalization process: an effective exciton temperature drops from 171.8 to 99.3 K within 1.2 ps through rapid intra-manifold population redistribution, followed by a slower recovery toward quasi-equilibrium mediated by exciton--environment interactions. Kullback--Leibler divergence analysis confirms the progressive transition from a non-equilibrium to a Boltzmann-distributed population. Our results establish state-resolved access to moiré exciton dynamics and provide a foundation for exploring nonequilibrium many-body phenomena in moiré quantum materials.

</details>


### [7] [Dynamical Keldysh model for colored noise and finite relaxation rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08280)
*F. Z. Bendjeddou,D. V. Efremov,M. N. Kiselev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 将Keldysh模型从无限关联时间推广到任意关联时间1/γ的有色噪声，并解析求解非对易矩阵耦合的多分量噪声情形；有限记忆使赝能隙被填充、峰合并、谱线变窄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原Keldysh模型仅处理关联时间无限长的Gaussian噪声，而实际量子点中噪声具有有限记忆。已有结论是非对易矩阵耦合的多分量噪声会产生赝能隙(pseudogap)，但有限关联时间对该能隙结构的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 在Keldysh框架下，将Gaussian噪声的关联时间由无限大推广为有限的1/γ（有色噪声），允许噪声分量通过非对易矩阵耦合，并对该模型进行解析求解，考察谱函数随关联时间的变化。

Result: 有限记忆导致赝能隙被填充，赝能隙两侧的峰发生合并，同时整体谱线宽度变窄；当1/γ→∞时结果回到原Keldysh模型的无限关联时间极限。

Conclusion: 有限关联时间（有色噪声）是消除或软化赝能隙的关键因素，其效应可通过1/γ连续调控，为非对易噪声下量子点谱的刻画提供了更贴近实际的解析描述。

Abstract: The Keldysh model describes quantum dots subjected to Gaussian noise with infinite correlation time. For multi-component noise coupled through non-commuting matrices, the spectrum exhibits a pseudogap. We solve the model for coloured noise with any correlation time, $1/ γ$. Finite memory leads to the pseudogap being filled, its peaks merging and the line narrowing.

</details>


### [8] [Multi-Channel Non-Abelian Edge Pumping via Multi-Degeneracy Encircling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07594)
*Qingyang Mo,Jiacheng Sun,Zhongfu Li,Jiajun Li,Wange Song,Tao Li,Shuang Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在硅光芯片上通过绝热环绕多能带简并实现多通道非阿贝尔边缘泵浦，完成分处不同物理边界的三个拓扑边缘模式的编织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波系统中的非阿贝尔操作此前局限于局域体态或同一边界模式，且常依赖脆弱的带隙闭合简并；传统Thouless泵仅支持跨单一带隙的两通道边缘传输。

Method: 构造最小三能带"能带三角"，由全局能带连通性连接两个相反电荷的外尔点与一个零电荷简并，产生异边界与同边界两类边缘通道；依序切换通道，使三个位于不同物理边界的拓扑边缘模式发生非阿贝尔编织。

Result: 实验实现多通道非阿贝尔边缘泵浦，全过程保持完全带隙，其保护源于拓扑绕数而非维持谱简并；该能带三角原理可系统性推广至任意通道数。

Conclusion: 为集成光子电路中拓扑态的集体、序列可控路由提供可扩展方案。

Abstract: Non-Abelian operations enable sequence-dependent control of light, yet experimental realizations in wave systems have remained confined to local bulk or same-boundary modes and have often required fragile gap-closing degeneracies. Conventional Thouless pumps, meanwhile, support only two-channel edge transfer across a single gap. Here we demonstrate multi-channel non-Abelian edge pumping on a silicon photonic chip by adiabatically encircling multiple band degeneracies in a multi-gap lattice. In a minimal three-band band-triangle, two opposite-charge Weyl points and one zero-charge degeneracy are linked by global band connectivity, yielding both opposite-boundary and same-boundary edge channels. Sequencing these channels produces non-Abelian braiding of three topologically protected edge modes that reside on distinct physical boundaries. The process remains fully gapped and is protected by topological winding rather than by maintained spectral degeneracies. The same band-triangle principle extends systematically to an arbitrary number of channels, establishing a scalable route to collective, sequence-controlled routing of topological states in integrated photonic circuits.

</details>


### [9] [Nonlinear Interaction and Topological Selection Rules in two Skyrmion Merging: Emergence of Skyrmionium Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07807)
*M. Al Busaidi,T. Hakioglu,R. Sbiaa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过解析建模与LLG微磁学模拟，研究两个Néel斯格明子的合并动力学，揭示出形成稳定skyrmionium的拓扑选择规则：只有同极性、反绕数的组合在中等重叠时形成skyrmionium，其余构型均弛豫为单一斯格明子；并给出skyrmionium坍缩的临界半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧密相邻磁斯格明子之间的非线性相互作用认识不足，尤其是合并为单一斯格明子还是稳定skyrmionium的判据尚不明确，缺乏可预测的控制框架。

Method: 采用解析建模结合基于Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert动力学的微磁学模拟，通过改变初始间距、极性、绕数及斯格明子尺寸，考察交换能、Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用、各向异性与静磁能的影响，并进行能量分析。

Result: 发现拓扑选择规则：同极性、反绕数的斯格明子在中等重叠度下形成稳定skyrmionium，其他构型均弛豫为单一斯格明子；存在临界半径，低于该半径skyrmionium坍缩，说明其形成需兼具合适拓扑与最小尺寸。能量分析表明转变源于交换阻挫的协同弛豫以及DM能与静磁能的重分布，形成双畴壁结构；所得skyrmionium在平衡半径附近呈现持续的呼吸振荡，确认其作为亚稳态的动力学稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究建立了控制斯格明子合并的预测性框架，并为在手性磁纳米结构中工程设计skyrmionium织构提供了设计准则。

We examine the effects of exchange, Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction, anisotropy, and magnetostatic energies by varying the initial separation, polarity, winding number, and skyrmion size. Our results reveal a topological selection rule: skyrmions with identical polarity and opposite winding numbers form a stable skyrmionium at intermediate overlap, while all other configurations relax into a single skyrmion. We also identify a critical radius below which the skyrmionium collapses, showing that its formation requires both the appropriate topology and a minimum size. Energy analysis reveals that the transition involves cooperative relaxation of exchange frustration and redistribution of Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya and magnetostatic energies, leading to a double-domain-wall structure. The resulting skyrmionium exhibits persistent breathing oscillations around an equilibrium radius, confirming its dynamical stability as a metastable state. These findings establish a predictive framework for controlling skyrmion merging and provide design guidelines for engineering skyrmionium textures in chiral magnetic nanostructures.

Abstract: Chiral anomaly is a fundamental manifestation of topological matter caused by chirality imbalance. It can be of great practical value by providing a mechanism for dissipationless transport. In contrast to the 2D material, 3D Gr/Sr/SiC exhibits a signature of the chiral anomaly - quadratic negative magnetoresistance in parallel magnetic and electric fields. Also, the functional form of the temperature dependence of the magnetoresistance agrees with the chiral anomaly. Abstract: Hexagonal germanium combines direct-gap optical activity with the low-nuclear-spin environment of a group-IV material, making it a candidate platform for spin qubits and spin-photon interfaces. Using a ten-band $\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}$ model, we study the response of confined hole states in hexagonal germanium nanowires to magnetic and electric fields. We show that, by symmetry, the top valence band of the bulk crystal has a vanishing $g$ factor in the plane perpendicular to the $c$ axis, in analogy with the in-plane $g$ factor of heavy holes in two-dimensional hole gases. In nanowires, confinement induces only small transverse $g$-tensor components, so the $g$ tensor remains strongly anisotropic. A transverse electric field strongly reduces the nanowire band gap, providing a gate-tunable optical signature and limiting the usable field range. The same field induces a Rashba spin-orbit interaction that is markedly stronger for fields perpendicular to the $c$ axis and depends nonlinearly on the field strength. In narrow wires at strong fields, the spin-orbit length reaches a few tens of nanometers, comparable to hole devices in cubic silicon. We identify the microscopic origin of the Rashba coupling as a second-order process mediated by a few intermediate subbands with distinct symmetry selection rules. Our results provide guidance for the design of electrically controlled hole-spin qubits in hexagonal germanium nanowires.

Conclusion: 该体系实现了磁态的可写入与读取双重功能，为二聚体基复杂磁性材料及自旋电子学应用铺平实验道路。

Abstract: We present a method to create metal-organic dimer complexes by on-surface assembly on a metal substrate using low-temperature scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) and spectroscopy (STS). We demonstrate that a dimer of Mn-Phthalocyanine (MnPc)2 on a Ag(111) surface can be switched between two stable configurations upon a small conformational change controlled by STM manipulation. By means of density-functional theory calculations, it is found that the two conformations correspond to an antiferromagnetic (AFM) and a ferromagnetic (FM) state respectively. Directly coordinated Mn atoms of the dimer lead to an AFM-coupling whereas indirectly coordinated (shifted) Mn atoms lead to a FM-coupling. Rarely seen in a molecular- dimers with transition-metal atoms, the FM-AFM-FM transition is thus readily on-surface accessible. Furthermore, the two configurations of the switch are easily identified by their Kondo states, opening interesting routes in terms of both, writing (FM versus AFM states) and reading. These results pave the experimental route toward dimer-based materials with complex magnetic structures of potential interest for application in spintronics, logics and computing.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验传统Thouless泵浦框架（基于动量空间拓扑不变量）能否直接推广到被输运量不守恒的各向异性自旋链体系。

Method: 对含可调面内各向异性的二聚化自旋-1/2 XY链作Jordan-Wigner变换，映射为扩展Rice-Mele型费米子模型；结合动量空间能带与陈数分析，并在开边界条件下做实时空演化（实空间诊断）。

Result: 动量空间存在陈数为±1的能隙区域及平庸、无能隙区域，预示量子化Thouless型自旋泵浦；但实空间演化显示，除各向同性极限外输运一般非量子化——输运子空间本征态在驱动周期中发生杂化，输运依赖驱动协议细节。仅在各向同性极限（解耦的Rice-Mele扇区、被输运自旋分量守恒）下量子化泵浦与输运才得以保留。

Conclusion: 动量空间拓扑不变量本身不足以保证各向异性自旋链中的量子化自旋输运；当被输运量不守恒时传统Thouless泵浦框架不能直接适用，凸显实空间诊断在拓扑输运现象中的必要性。

Abstract: We investigate the prospect of topological spin pumping and transport in a dimerized spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ anisotropic XY chain with tunable in-plane anisotropy. Through a Jordan-Wigner transformation, the system maps onto an extended Rice-Mele-type fermionic model whose momentum-space band structure exhibits gapped regions characterized by nonzero Chern numbers ($\pm 1$) as well as trivial and gapless regimes. Conventional momentum-space analysis therefore predicts quantized Thouless-type spin pumping in parts of the Chern number diagram. However, real-space time evolution under open boundary conditions reveals that the spin transport is generally not quantized away from the isotropic limit. In the anisotropic regime, eigen-energy states within the transporting subspace hybridize during the driving cycle, leading to a transport that depends on details of the driving protocol. Quantized pumping and transport survive only in the isotropic limit, where the Hamiltonian reduces to decoupled Rice-Mele sectors and the transported spin component is conserved. Our results demonstrate that momentum-space topological invariants alone do not guarantee quantized spin transport in anisotropic spin chains. More broadly, they show that the conventional Thouless-pumping framework does not straightforwardly extend to situations where the transported quantity is not conserved, highlighting the importance of real-space diagnostics in topological transport phenomena.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文在300 mm晶圆级自旋轨道矩驱动的磁隧道结人工突触中演示并调谐了多达8个电阻态，并评估其在神经网络推理中的精度与器件涨落影响。

Motivation: 基于MRAM的自旋电子突触器件是模拟神经形态架构中快速发展的方向，但如何在尺寸缩小的MRAM中获得提升推理精度所需的多权重（模拟）行为仍是难题。

Method: 在300 mm晶圆级SOT驱动的磁隧道结中，通过改变面内磁场诱导畴壁成核与钉扎产生多权重电阻态；采用迭代编程算法寻址目标电阻；结合电磁仿真分析机制；并将实测器件接入神经网络在MNIST上评估推理精度。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了面向多权重人工突触MRAM的实现路径，为基于自旋电子学的可扩展神经形态系统奠定基础；同时表明器件间一致性对精度至关重要，是后续需重点解决的问题。

Abstract: Spintronic synaptic devices based on magnetic random access memory (MRAM) are a rapidly evolving class within the analog neuromorphic architecture. However, obtaining the multi-weight or analog behavior desired for improved neural network inference accuracy in scaled MRAM remains a challenge. In this study, we demonstrate and tune up to eight distinct resistance levels in 300 mm wafer-scale magnetic tunnel junction artificial synapses driven by spin orbit torque. By varying the applied in-plane field, multi-weight resistance states emerge through domain wall nucleation and pinning. These multi-weight states appear stochastically and can be addressed with an iterative programming algorithm to program a specific resistance. Electromagnetic simulations indicate the multiple states are due to domain wall nucleation mediated by in-plane field and applied voltage. Implementation of the measured multi-weight devices in a neural network yields a 96.1% inference accuracy using the MNIST dataset if all devices provide the same number of multi-weight states. When experimental device-to-device variation is included, the accuracy is 48.6%. Overall, this work demonstrates a route towards multi-weight artificial synapse MRAM, establishing a path towards scalable neuromorphic systems based on spintronics.

</details>

### [15] [Langmuir kinetics and totally asymmetric volume exclusion in linear chains via Fock space formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07053)
*Luis D. S. Silva,Arthur M. S. Carvalho,Francisco A. G. Almeida,Gerson C. Duarte-Filho*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文用精确的Doi–Peliti算符形式与双正交本征向量分解，直接由准哈密顿量核求得有限TASEP-LK链（k≤16）的稳态，无需完整对角化。结果显示有限尺寸涨落抹平畴壁、产生连续相变交叉，慢动力学区平均场低估联合占据概率达50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TASEP-LK等驱动-扩散系统是交通流、传送网络与介观通道输运的基础模型；但有限链的精确稳态通常依赖计算代价高昂的完整矩阵对角化或时间演化矩阵指数，难以对多参数区间做系统扫描，且平均场对有限尺寸涨落与空间关联的刻画不足。

Method: 在Fock空间中采用精确Doi-Peliti场论算符表述；针对非对称非厄米矩阵引入双正交本征向量分解，从准哈密顿量的核（零空间）直接计算定态，绕开完整对角化与矩阵指数；对k≤16的有限线性链做精确离散计算、全局参数扫描、有限尺寸标度分析与两点关联（累积量）计算。

Result: 精确离散结果显示：有限尺寸随机涨落自然抹平奇异畴壁，密度剖面光滑、相变呈连续交叉；有限尺寸标度表明局域剖面随k→∞系统性地趋陡，收敛于连续场论预言的锐利畴壁；两点关联分析发现在慢动力学区，空间涨落产生强的格点间累积量，使标准平均场系统性地低估联合占据概率约50%。

Conclusion: 准哈密顿量核方法为介观输运提供了计算可行且精确的解析-数值框架；对于局域随机涨落与空间关联主导稳态的介观输运系统与短交通走廊，必须采用超越平均场、考虑有限尺寸效应的分析方法。

Abstract: Driven-diffusive systems combining spatial exclusion and bulk exchange, such as the totally asymmetric simple exclusion process coupled with Langmuir kinetics (TASEP-LK), serve as foundational models for particle transport in vehicular traffic, conveyor networks, and mesoscopic flow channels. In this work, we employ an exact Doi-Peliti field-theoretic operator formulation in Fock space to evaluate steady-state observables, continuous phase landscapes, and spatial correlations in finite linear chains up to $k = 16$. By introducing a decomposition in terms of biorthonormal eigenvectors for asymmetric non-Hermitian matrices, the stationary state is calculated directly from the kernel of the quasi-Hamiltonian, completely bypassing the computationally prohibitive complexity of full matrix diagonalization and time-dependent matrix exponentiation. Across distinct rate regimes and global parametric scans, our exact discrete calculations demonstrate that finite-size stochastic fluctuations naturally smear out singular domain walls, producing smooth density profiles and continuous phase crossovers. Finite-size scaling illustrates how local profiles systematically steepen toward the sharp domain wall predicted by continuum field theory as $k \to \infty$. Furthermore, two-point correlation analysis reveals that in slow-kinetics regimes, spatial fluctuations generate strong inter-site cumulants, causing standard mean-field approximations to systematically underestimate joint occupancy probabilities by 50\%. These findings emphasize the necessity of analytical methods for modeling mesoscopic transport systems and short traffic corridors where local stochastic fluctuations and spatial correlations dominate the stationary dynamics.

</details>


### [16] [Percolation of discorectangles oriented predominantly along two mutually perpendicular directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07484)
*Yuri Yu. Tarasevich*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究有限厚度碟矩形（sports stadium）在“马耳他十字”角度分布下、沿两垂直主方向的渗流，用排除面积+秩1修正的解析方法估计阈值，并由蒙特卡洛验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅处理零厚度棒（sticks）的渗流阈值，缺乏对具有有限厚度、端部呈半圆的碟矩形（discorectangle）物体的系统分析；需厘清厚度（几何加性项）如何影响绝对阈值与归一化曲线。

Method: 基于已知的碟矩形对排除面积解析式，采用特征值法估计渗流阈值；将连通矩阵分解为“棒矩阵 + 由排除面积常数部分 C=4lw+πw² 决定的秩1项”，导出 λ_max^DR ≈ λ_max^stick + C 与 n_c^DR ≈ n_c^stick/(1+C n_c^stick)；并用单位长度、长宽比 8/16/32 的碟矩形蒙特卡洛模拟（不同域尺寸、周期边界、union–find 标度分析）进行检验。

Result: 得到两个近似解析关系；厚度使绝对阈值平移，影响第一种归一化；而以最大特征值表述的第二种归一化抵消了加性几何贡献，仅保留取向依赖性，从而解释了归一化曲线的普适性。蒙特卡洛结果与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 有限厚度对渗流的影响可视为对零厚度棒结果的加性几何修正；取向依赖性与普适归一化行为保持不变，解析估计与数值模拟吻合。

Abstract: The percolation of finite-thickness elongated objects---discorectangles (stadiums)---oriented predominantly along two mutually perpendicular directions is studied, extending recent results for zero-thickness sticks. The angular distribution is the ``Maltese cross'' parametrized by $α$. Based on the known analytic expression for the excluded area of a discorectangle pair, the percolation threshold is estimated by the eigenvalue method. The discorectangle connectivity matrix is represented as the stick matrix plus a rank-1 term determined by the constant part of the excluded area, $C=4lw+πw^{2}$, which gives the approximate relations $λ_{\max}^{\mathrm{DR}}\approxλ_{\max}^{\mathrm{stick}}+C$ and $n_c^{\mathrm{DR}}\approx n_c^{\mathrm{stick}}/(1+C n_c^{\mathrm{stick}})$. Finite thickness shifts the absolute thresholds and affects the first normalization, whereas the second normalization, written through maximum eigenvalues, cancels the additive geometric contribution and preserves only the orientational dependence, explaining the universality of the normalized curves. Monte Carlo simulations of unit-length discorectangles with aspect ratios 8, 16, and 32, various domain sizes, periodic boundary conditions, and union--find scaling analysis confirm the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [17] [Electrical conductivity of a two-dimensional two-component medium: discretization and effective medium approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07491)
*Yuri Yu. Tarasevich*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

Neighboring cells are connected by an equivalent resistor whose conductivity is $σ_1$, $σ_2$, or the harmonic mean $σ_{12}=2σ_1σ_2/(σ_1+σ_2)$, with probabilities $(1-p)^2$, $p^2$, and $2p(1-p)$, respectively. Applying Foster's theorem and the EMA yields a closed equation for the dimensionless effective conductivity $x$ and, after transformation, a cubic equation whose coefficients depend on $p$ and the conductivity ratio $r$. The EMA predictions are compared with direct calculations on a $200\times200$ resistor network with periodic lateral boundary conditions and Dirichlet conditions on the top and bottom. For uncorrelated disorder, good agreement is found. For correlated disorder, when resistor conductivities are assigned according to the discretization rule from the colors of adjacent nodes, significant deviations appear and the percolation-like inflection point shifts to the right of $1/2$. The results question the validity of the correlated-disorder EMA approach and help explain discrepancies previously reported for random nanowire networks.

Abstract: The dynamics of type I excitability neurons near the bifurcation point are governed by the model of quadratic integrate-and-fire neuron (QIF). Abstract: In two dimensions, the ordered phase of constant-density flocks is described by a single angle field, which makes them arguably the simplest systems with a scale-free ordered phase of collective motion. Abstract: Masked diffusion models generate text by reversing a stochastic masking process. Unlike autoregressive LLMs, which generate text one token at a time, they can reveal many tokens in parallel, at the price of an error, since tokens revealed together are drawn independently. We show that this trade-off maps onto the statistical physics of driven many-particle systems. (i) For any data, the error of parallel generation is the entropy production of the generation process relative to the reversed masking process. (ii) For Markov data, generation is a random sequential adsorption process with labelled gaps, which we solve exactly for any number of steps. (iii) For many steps, the error becomes the dissipation of a slowly driven system: the unmasking schedule plays the role of the driving protocol, the number of steps that of its duration, and the error is set by a thermodynamic metric that we obtain in closed form from the mutual information between tokens. Optimal schedules are geodesics of this metric. The exact solution shows how correlations in the text shape both the error and the quality of the output. The longer the correlations, the more the error concentrates at the start of generation. Schedules that start with small steps then gain more, both in path error and in perplexity, and need fewer steps to reach their asymptotic behaviour.

</details>

Abstract: The square-lattice Ising model is exactly solvable in zero magnetic field, but the scope of exact solutions at nonzero field remains unresolved. We eliminate these exceptions using Galois conjugation and the large-order growth of droplets of reversed spins. At conjugate fugacities outside the unit circle, nearly square droplets produce coefficient growth incompatible with an algebraic differential equation. We prove that cancellations cannot remove this growth in the full pressure series. The free-energy result also holds for the Lieb lattice and square lattices with equal-length chain decorations and uniform coupling and field. In every dimension $d\ge2$, we additionally resolve fields for which $e^{-2|H|}$ has a real algebraic conjugate greater than one.

Abstract: Thermodynamic curvature is widely used as a diagnostic of interactions and phase transitions, yet its interpretation is usually tested only in the thermodynamic limit. Small spin clusters are physically relevant in their own right, since a macroscopic sample of independent molecular trimers or a few-qubit quantum simulator is governed exactly by the few-spin partition function. They also provide the only setting in which the geometry can be obtained in closed form together with its full dependence on the interaction topology. We study the Fisher-Rao (Ruppeiner) geometry of three Ising spins with arbitrary pairwise couplings in a longitudinal field. This cluster interpolates continuously between an open chain and a geometrically frustrated triangle. For the symmetric triangle we obtain the scalar curvature in closed form. It vanishes on the noninteracting line and on a line of fixed field-to-temperature ratio that does not depend on the coupling, which coincides with the inflection point of the single-spin susceptibility. The near-zero behaviour is therefore exactly linear and reflects analyticity rather than critical scaling. In the frustrated antiferromagnetic regime the curvature remains finite and negative down to zero temperature. A general low-temperature formula relates this plateau to the change in magnetization content between the degenerate ground manifold and its first excitation. Any bond anisotropy destroys the plateau below a crossover temperature that depends only logarithmically on the anisotropy, so the curvature is an extremely sensitive detector of exact ground-state degeneracy. On the ferromagnetic side the zero-temperature curvature is universal for all couplings, whereas additional zero lines in anisotropic clusters track field-induced crossings of excited levels.

The overall structure of the asymptotic expansion depends on the number of Fermi points, those points for which the quasi-momentum distribution takes the value $1/2$. Abstract: Quantum algorithms for elastic media simulation have been conjectured to offer up to a quartic quantum advantage over classical methods; however, the devil is always in the details. Here, we determine key physical conditions under which this quartic separation could be achieved in the relevant parameters. For elastic lattice systems, we define microscopically resolved bulk observables (MRBOs) as those that can be efficiently extracted from a quantum state, but cannot be resolved by a known continuum model with fewer degrees of freedom than the lattice. We then argue that the estimation of MRBOs is a candidate problem for quartic quantum advantage in problem size. We propose MRBOs for pseudorandom heterogeneous materials as an explicit end-to-end application where this occurs, while admitting efficient `quantum cheese' encodings of the material properties. We thus demonstrate how the interplay between regularity, physical scale, observable sensitivity and quantum encodability constrains the relation between classical and quantum complexity.

Conclusion: 非局域魔的线性增长源于高斯局域幺正的限制，而非自由费米子态的本质。由于自由费米子对的纠缠谱高度简并（C_E仅线性增长），一般局域非高斯幺正即可大幅削减非局域魔至对数级。因此，自由费米子（包括Hartree–Fock与BCS平均场态）的几乎全部魔都是局域的。

If these local unitaries are restricted to Gaussian ones, the nonlocal magic grows linearly with the number $m$ of partially entangled fermion pairs. We show that this growth is an artifact of the Gaussian restriction: local Gaussian unitaries minimize the nonlocal stabilizer Rényi entropy for up to three pairs, but not from four pairs on. General local non-Gaussian unitaries that respect fermion parity take $m$ free-fermion pairs to fermionic Bell pairs with a fidelity that decays at most as $1/\sqrt m$ compared to exponential decay with Gaussian unitaries, so the nonlocal magic drops from linear to logarithmic in $m$. The reason is statistical --- Bell pairs have equal Schmidt weights, that is, a fully degenerate entanglement spectrum, and a state can stay far from them only if its Schmidt weights are spread over many orders of magnitude. This spread is measured by the capacity of entanglement $C_E$, the variance of the entanglement Hamiltonian, and the nonlocal magic of any fermionic state grows at most logarithmically with $C_E$. Free-fermion pairs add to $C_E$ independently, so $C_E$ grows only linearly in $m$, and this leads to the stabilizer fidelity decreasing at most as $1/\sqrt m$. Almost all the magic of free fermions, including Hartree--Fock and BCS mean-field states, is therefore local.

</details>

The bound is sharp, so the accuracy improves as the anisotropy grows. Second, in the computational basis, a subspace that misses a single node of a flux surface suppresses the flux-function component there as $χ_\parallel^{-1/2}$, which gives an error floor when the limiting solution is nonzero on that surface. At $χ_\parallel/χ_\perp=10^9$, the computational basis gives an error of order unity for every shot budget, whereas a quantum Fourier transform on the poloidal register gives $1.3\times10^{-5}$. The same barrier limits sample-based quantum diagonalization. The aligned measurement needs no time evolution, so the circuit is only state preparation and a five-qubit quantum Fourier transform. On a ten-qubit superconducting processor, the aligned basis gives a median error of $7.0\times10^{-6}$ with 768 shots, whereas the computational basis gives errors above 0.94 up to $1.2\times10^4$ shots.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无阻挫性（基态同时最小化所有局域相互作用项）在量子多体物理中具有重要价值，AKLT模型与Kitaev环面码是其代表。核心问题是：给定哈密顿量是否对所有系统尺寸都无阻挫。作者希望厘清该判定问题的可判定性与可证明性边界。

Method: 从数学逻辑与可计算性角度分析该判定问题：一方面构造与哈密顿量无关的有限系统尺寸截断，证明固定局域维数下无阻挫性可判定；另一方面构造无阻挫哈密顿量，论证其无阻挫性在ZFC中不可证明；再通过分析截断作为局域维数函数的不可计算性来调和两者。

Result: (1) 对任意固定局域希尔伯特空间维数，无阻挫性可判定：存在有限且与哈密顿量无关的尺寸截断，使该截断尺寸处的无阻挫性保证所有尺寸的无阻挫性；(2) 存在无阻挫哈密顿量，其无阻挫性无法在ZFC标准公理内证明；(3) 该尺寸截断虽对每个维数有限，但作为维数的函数不可计算。

Conclusion: 无阻挫性判定问题展现出可判定性与可证明性之间的意外根本性区分：可判定并不意味着在标准数学公理内可证明。这为量子多体物理中算法可判定性与逻辑可证明性的关系提供了新的基础性认识。

Abstract: The frustration-free property is valuable in quantum many-body systems, which states that its ground states simultaneously minimize all local interaction terms, even when these terms act on overlapping degrees of freedom. Prominent examples include the AKLT model and Kitaev's toric code model, which serve as paradigmatic models of symmetry-protected topological phases and topological order, respectively. A fundamental question is whether a given Hamiltonian is frustration-free for all system sizes. Here we establish two seemingly contradictory results concerning this decision problem. First, we prove that frustration-freeness is decidable for any fixed local Hilbert space dimension. Specifically, there exists a finite, Hamiltonian-independent system-size cutoff such that frustration-freeness at this cutoff size guarantees frustration-freeness at all system sizes. Second, on the other hand, we also show that there exists a frustration-free Hamiltonian whose frustration-freeness cannot be proved within the standard axioms of mathematics (ZFC). We reconcile these seemingly contradictory results by showing that the cutoff, although finite for each local Hilbert space dimension, is uncomputable as a function of that dimension. Our findings reveal an unexpected fundamental distinction between decidability and provability arising in quantum many-body physics.

Abstract: We introduce dithered belief-propagation decoding for circuit-level quantum memory to mitigate the oscillations and trapping behavior that limit standard belief-propagation (BP) decoders for qLDPC codes. Our approach combines a quantum-adapted sign-flip mechanism with chained randomized prior perturbation. The proposed decoder achieves accuracy comparable to or better than state-of-the-art Relay-BP, especially at higher physical error rates, while trading a moderate increase in additions for about 98% fewer multiplications on average and eliminating node-level RNGs. These results suggest that dithering is a promising approach toward real-time decoding of quantum memories.

Abstract: Proposals to detect gravitationally induced entanglement between massive particles offer a promising pathway toward probing the quantum nature of gravity. We perform a systematic geometric optimization and comparative analysis of Stern--Gerlach (SG) and Mach--Zehnder (MZ) matter-wave interferometers for this purpose, deriving exact analytical expressions for the gravitational phase of each configuration. Our first key result is that geometric optimization is indispensable. For commonly assumed experimental parameters, neglecting the optimization of geometric ratios can suppress the accumulated phase, and hence the detectability of entanglement, by up to several orders of magnitude. Second, we provide a complete comparative analysis of two SG orientations (linear and parallel) and three MZ orientations (translation-symmetric, mirror-symmetric, and parallel-arms). Abstract: Whether useful structured hardness can arise from unstructured assumptions is a fundamental question in complexity and cryptography. The longstanding Bennett-Gill question asks whether $\mathsf{NP}\cap\mathsf{coNP}$ remains hard relative to a random oracle; [GIKKLS, STOC 2023] recently gave a conditional affirmative answer in the classical setting. We establish the quantum analogue: if $\mathsf{UP}\not\subseteq\mathsf{BQP}$, then $\mathsf{NP}^{O}\cap\mathsf{coNP}^{O}\not\subseteq\mathsf{BQP}^{O}$ with probability $1$ over a random oracle $O$.

Our conditional oracle separation provides evidence that such structured quantum hardness can nevertheless arise from unstructured sources.

This barrier excludes a broad class of black-box approaches to deriving structured quantum hardness from generic cryptographic primitives, providing partial evidence that the non-black-box techniques in our first result are essential.

### [57] [Elimination of quantum causal confusion in the presence of dual states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07449)

TL;DR: 论文研究"量子因果混淆"——存在一类二分量子关联，既可由并行（空间）测量产生，也可由时序（时间）测量产生。作者证明：只要对偶态的约化密度算符是"斜的"（Pauli 展开系数全非零），则两种场景下的联合概率必然不同，时间场景联合概率违反无信号条件，从而消除因果混淆；且斜态在 Bloch 球中构成开稠密集，故消除混淆是常态而非例外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期发现存在大量二分量子关联可同时与空间（并行）和时序（顺序）测量相容，这被称为量子因果混淆，其数学刻画为"对偶态"。核心疑问是：对偶态对应的空间与时间场景在因果层面是否本质不可区分。作者希望厘清这种混淆在何种条件下成立、在何种条件下消失，从而界定因果混淆的适用范围。

Method: 引入"斜态"（oblique state）概念：对偶态的约化密度算符在 Pauli 基展开下所有系数均非零。分析空间与时间两种场景下的联合概率结构，比较二者是否一致，并考察时间场景联合概率是否满足无信号（no-signalling）条件。

Result: 当约化密度算符为斜态时，导致因果模糊关联的底层联合概率在空间与时间场景中不同；具体地，时间联合概率本质上违反无信号条件，因此量子因果混淆被消除。此外，斜态在 Bloch 球中构成开稠密子集，说明消除量子因果混淆是普遍情形而非特例。

Conclusion: 量子因果混淆是脆弱的、非泛型的现象：仅在约化态为非斜（部分 Pauli 系数为零）的测度零集合上可能成立，而对几乎所有对偶态，空间与时间场景在因果上可区分。这为"空间—时间关联能否同等看待"给出了否定性、且具普适性的回答。

Abstract: There is a rich class of bipartite quantum correlations between qubits which are consistent both with measurements performed in parallel and measurements performed in sequence. This phenomenon has recently been termed \emph{quantum causal confusion}, which is made mathematically precise by the notion of a dual state---a bipartite density operator whose associated correlations are causally ambiguous. A natural question is then whether or not the spatial and temporal scenarios associated with a dual state are fundamentally indistinguishable from a causal perspective. Here we show that as long as the reduced density operator of a dual state is oblique---meaning that all coefficients in its Pauli expansion are non-zero---then the underlying joint probabilities giving rise to such causally ambiguous correlations will differ in the spatial and temporal scenarios. Specifically, the temporal joint probabilities will inherently violate the no-signalling condition, thus eliminating quantum causal confusion. Moreover, as oblique states form an open dense subset of the Bloch ball, the elimination of quantum causal confusion is the norm rather than an exception.

</details>

Abstract: We explore a minimal phenomenological model of coupling between the geometry of the classical spacetime and the quantum state space. Assuming a simple structure of the combined manifold, bilinear interaction between the corresponding geometric scalars and the simplest case of the two-qubit entanglement dynamics, we derive an illustrative upper bound on the geometric coupling constant from the requirement of the Clauser-Horne-Shimony-Holt (CHSH) inequality violation over a finite time interval.

</details>

*Haihui Wang,Yongming Li*

TL;DR: 该论文建立了算子稳定子Rényi熵(OSE)的标度行为与Heisenberg算子能否被高效Pauli截断近似之间的严格判据：体积律OSE(α≥1)阻止高效截断，对数增长OSE(0<α<1/2)保证对所有态一致可近似，特定系综下可延拓至0<α<1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算子魔性（operator magic）被认为是Heisenberg算子可压缩性的诊断量，但缺乏"OSE标度在何种条件下真正决定Pauli基截断可行性"的严格理论刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白并为经典模拟方法提供指导。

Method: 采用严格解析证明：以Pauli截断近似期望值为框架，将OSE的标度（体积律 vs. 对数增长）与截断误差界关联；给出反例说明仅凭OSE标度无法扩展充分区间；引入低平均态系综与独立符号假设，并推导迭代Pauli传播的累积误差界及固定阶OTOC的近似保证。

Result: 证明α≥1时OSE体积律导致在足够小固定误差下无法对所有态高效Pauli截断；0<α<1/2对数增长保证对所有输入态一致高效可近似；反例表明该充分区间不能仅靠OSE标度扩展；限制到合适低平均态系综时可延拓至0<α<1；在独立符号假设下，0<α<1对数OSE以高概率保证指定精度的高效截断；并给出迭代Pauli传播的累积误差界与OTOC近似保证。

Conclusion: OSE标度可作为算子Pauli截断可近似性的定量判据，明确了算子魔性何时制约、何时促成高效经典模拟，为基于Pauli截断/传播的经典算法设计与适用性判断提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Operator stabilizer Rényi entropy (OSE) quantifies the magic of Heisenberg operators and serves as a diagnostic of their compressibility in the Pauli basis. We establish rigorous bounds showing when an operator can be efficiently approximated by Pauli truncation in terms of the scaling of its OSE. Specifically, we prove that volume-law scaling of OSE for $α\geq1$ prevents efficient Pauli truncation from reproducing expectation values for all states at sufficiently small fixed error. On the other hand, logarithmic growth of OSE for $0<α<1/2$ guarantees efficient Pauli truncation approximability uniformly over all states. A counterexample shows that this sufficient range cannot be extended on the basis of OSE scaling alone. If we restrict to correlations over suitable low-average state ensembles, then the sufficient range extends to $0<α<1$. We further derive cumulative error bounds for iterative Pauli propagation and approximation guarantees for fixed-order out-of-time-ordered correlators. Moreover, under an independent-sign assumption, we show that logarithmic OSE for $0<α<1$ guarantees efficient Pauli truncation with the prescribed accuracy for all input states with high probability. Our results provide a theoretical framework for understanding when operator magic constrains or enables efficient Pauli truncation and for guiding the development of classical simulation methods.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 量子控制可提升传感精度，但实际协议受探针制备与可用控制资源限制；需在这些约束下优化协议，以确定实验上可达到的精度。

Result: 两个自旋1/2示例：横向场传感中，时变控制优于优化后的常数控制；在强有色噪声下的交流场传感中，优化波形优于CPMG脉冲序列。

Conclusion: 在可用控制资源范围内优化传感协议，对提升实验可达到的精度具有重要意义。

Abstract: Quantum control can enhance sensing precision, but practical protocols are constrained by probe preparation and the available control resources. Optimizing the sensing protocol within these constraints helps establish what precision is experimentally attainable. We study quantum metrology from a fixed initial probe state under restricted Hamiltonian controls with bounded amplitudes. Building on Pontryagin's maximum principle, we formulate quantum Fisher information maximization at a prescribed interrogation time for multiple control channels subject to a weighted amplitude constraint. The formulation also accommodates stochastic Hamiltonian evolution and time-local master equations. A geometric description of sensitivity accumulation reveals the mechanisms underlying the optimized protocols. Two spin-$1/2$ examples demonstrate the resulting enhancement: time-dependent control outperforms optimized constant control in transverse-field sensing, while optimized waveforms improve upon CPMG pulses for AC-field sensing under strong colored noise. These findings highlight the importance of optimizing sensing protocols within available control resources to improve experimentally attainable precision.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 整数缠绕数在零温下可分类手征对称保护的拓扑相，但推广到热混合态存在困难；Uhlmann相位作为Berry相位的混合态推广提供了几何框架，却仅取两个量子化值，无法分辨高缠绕数对应的多个热区间。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了与混合态几何相位并存的整数值拓扑刻画，为有限温度拓扑相分类提供了实验可行的新判据。

Abstract: Integer winding numbers provide a well-established classification of topological phases protected by chiral symmetry at zero temperature, but extending this classification to thermal mixed states remains challenging. The Uhlmann phase, a mixed-state extension of the Berry phase, provides a geometric framework for characterizing finite-temperature topology. However, this geometric phase is restricted to two quantized values and therefore cannot resolve the multiple thermal regimes associated with high winding numbers. Here, we experimentally observe integer mixed-state winding numbers in a photonic quantum-walk chip. We prepare and reconstruct thermal states associated with high-winding chiral Hamiltonians and their spectrum-preserving dimensional extensions. The reconstructed Uhlmann overlaps form closed trajectories in the complex plane, and their integer windings around the zero-overlap point are directly observed. As temperature increases, the Uhlmann winding number undergoes multiple discrete changes, accompanied by switches of the Uhlmann phase and the successive disappearance of momentum-resolved topological kinks. These results highlight an integer-valued topological characterization alongside the mixed-state geometric phase.

Abstract: The detection and quantification of quantum entanglement is a central issue in quantum information science, particularly for unknown quantum states. Based on symmetric measurement correlation matrices, we present a family of separability criteria that can be experimentally applied without state tomography, and recover some related existing criteria as special cases. Using the constructed correlation matrices, we further derive a class of lower bounds of concurrence. Through illustrative examples, we demonstrate that our criteria outperform some existing separability criteria in entanglement detection, and that the obtained lower bounds on concurrence are tighter than known counterparts.

Abstract: We propose a scheme for preparing spin-squeezed states in an ensemble of $N$ spin-$1/2$ particles based on Grover's algorithm. With only a few oracles, the scheme can exactly prepare superpositions of a few neighboring Dicke states with the Wineland squeezing parameter scaling as $N^{-1}$. The scheme requires only global rotations and Dicke-selective phase shifts, thus avoiding the need to engineer a dedicated many-body Hamiltonian for the squeezing dynamics. We further discuss the feasibility of realizing the scheme in cavity QED systems, with the phase-matched Grover iterations realized by single-photon reflection from a dispersively coupled cavity. Full-sequence scattering calculations accounting for finite photon bandwidth and loss show that appreciable spin squeezing can be achieved with high fidelity for suitable system parameters.

### [64] [Observation of Inter-Transition Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07768)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有理论预言开放量子系统中跃迁相干间的耗散耦合可产生一种独特的量子干涉（ITI），但缺乏实验验证。

Method: 对Kerr参量振荡器构成的超导人工原子进行相干微波反射谱测量；施加强参量驱动使两个跃迁接近简并并同时贡献反射响应；将独立跃迁模型与含耗散耦合的理论计算和实测谱线对比。

Conclusion: 实验证实了ITI的存在，表明跃迁相干间的耗散耦合能定性改变光谱响应，并将跃迁相干确立为开放量子系统中量子干涉的新研究平台。

Abstract: Many well-known quantum interference phenomena are associated with coherent superpositions of quantum states. Recent theoretical work showed that dissipative coupling between transition coherences can give rise to a distinct form of quantum interference in open quantum systems, termed inter-transition interference (ITI). Here we experimentally observe ITI through coherent microwave reflection spectroscopy of a superconducting artificial atom realized by a Kerr parametric oscillator. Using strong parametric driving, we access a regime in which two transitions become nearly degenerate and simultaneously contribute to the reflected response. The observed spectral lineshapes are not adequately captured by a model of independent transitions. By contrast, theoretical calculations that incorporate dissipative coupling between the corresponding transition coherences reproduce the essential features of the measured spectra. These results provide experimental evidence for ITI and demonstrate that dissipative coupling between transition coherences can qualitatively modify spectroscopic responses. More broadly, our work identifies transition coherences as a new arena for quantum interference in open quantum systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络的非经典行为同时受测量基选择与分布式源独立性的共同制约，需要可解析、可复现的框架来刻画并调节双局域(bilocal)网络中的非双局域关联。

Abstract: We introduce constrained quantum imaginary time evolution (CQITE), extending quantum imaginary time evolution (QITE) to constrained-search dynamics for ground-state preparation with prescribed expectation-value constraints. A central challenge in practical QITE is constructing a compact unitary representation of the imaginary-time evolution while maintaining accuracy and controlling the approximation error.

This characterization induces a hierarchy of operator directions expressed through nested anti-commutators. Truncating the hierarchy produces a systematic static operator pool, with polynomial size in the system size at any fixed truncation order. The resulting construction provides a principled route to compact unitary approximations of imaginary-time dynamics.

Such pulses are well suited for superconducting transmons as well as other forms of qubits embedded in a multi-level structure. By effectively reproducing a two-level dynamics within a multi-level system, this approach is versatile thanks to its compatibility with two-level control strategies under conditions (smoothness, duration) that are analyzed.

Abstract: We observe negative non-linear differential dissipation in a low-frequency (32.7 kHz) miniature quartz tuning fork, representing a high-Q piezoelectric resonator, at temperatures below 0.3 K. In the case of low-frequency resonators, the equivalent temperature of the mechanical mode is orders of magnitude lower than the thermodynamic temperature of the device, implying that accessible TTLS states are largely saturated. We demonstrate that the observed temperature and drive dependence of the losses fit well within the framework of an interacting TTLS bath, indicating the presence of a channel for energy transfer between the mechanical mode and thermally activated TTLSs. This approach enables an experimentally validated, fast (97 ns) and high-fidelity (99.40(9)%) CZ gate, where error analysis reveals that gate errors arise mainly from static decoherence. Abstract: Quantum processors remain susceptible to crosstalk-induced effects despite the continuous evolution of their underlying hardware architectures. The probability of a measurement outcome flip in the victim qubits reaches a maximum value of $60\%$. Also, it is seen that processors sharing identical coupling graphs exhibit different vulnerability profiles, indicating that connectivity alone is insufficient to predict attack efficiency. Finally, to demonstrate the practical relevance of these attacks, we study their impact on quantum random access codes (QRACs), observing a degradation in their average success probabilities.

Abstract: Resolving how first-order quantum coherence and definite measurement outcomes emerge from entangled vacuum fluctuations across spacelike-separated regions is unified here by the principle that quantum relative entropy governs the energy-information flux across causal boundaries [P.~Dorau and A.~Much, Phys.~Rev.~Lett.~\textbf{136}, 091602 (2026)]. We establish the exact quantum-optical realization of this principle using dual, spacelike-separated entangled nonlinear biphoton sources implemented in quantum electrodynamics waveguides pumped by an optical frequency comb and seeded by a continuous-wave laser in a coherent state $|α\rangle$. Tracing over the unmeasured idler continuum yields an exact spatial Lindblad master equation along the propagation axis, proving that spatial dephasing is driven by an information divergence flux determined by the SPACS overlap fidelity $F = |α|^2/(1+|α|^2)$. Uniting this relative-entropy flow with quotient measurement theory (detailed in Section~S3 of the Supplemental Material), we rigorously resolve nonlocal wavefunction collapse into local coset projections---explaining macroscopic state reduction without superluminal signaling. This exact open-system framework simultaneously saturates the wave-particle-entanglement triality [T.~H.~Yoon and M.~Cho, Sci.~Adv.~\textbf{7}, eabi9268 (2021)] and anchors hardware-native time--frequency Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill stabilizers to the comb symplectic structure.

Result: 最优控制使幺正strokes在随系统尺寸线性增长的时间尺度（而非线性驱动下的二次增长）上获得近理想功；解析模型预测两个竞争的功率最优区间：趋近理想功的慢分支，以及尺寸无关的快分支，后者在超过某交叉尺寸后占主导，且与数值最优控制结果定量一致；模型还给岀不同于功最优尺度的功率最优能量尺度，可进一步提升功率。

Abstract: In finite-time quantum Otto engines, maximizing power requires balancing the duration of the unitary strokes against the losses from nonadiabatic excitations. We address this trade-off in an Otto engine with a free-fermion working medium, using quantum optimal control. Having determined the single-particle energy scales that maximize ideal work at fixed reservoir temperatures, we realize them as the two endpoint Hamiltonians of a transverse-field Ising chain, connected by a path that crosses the quantum critical point. Optimal control of the unitary strokes yields near-ideal work on a time scale growing linearly with system size, instead of quadratically as for linear driving. We derive a time-rescaling relation that maps protocols with different energy scales onto a single reference problem and, for complete thermalization strokes of fixed duration, use it to build an analytical model of the cycle power. The model predicts two competing maximum-power regimes: a slow branch that approaches the ideal work, and a fast, size-independent branch that trades work for a shorter cycle and dominates above a crossover size, in quantitative agreement with numerical optimal-control results. It also identifies power-optimal energy scales that differ from the work-optimal ones and further increase the power. Our results provide a predictive approach to jointly optimizing the operating conditions and finite-time dynamics of quantum heat engines.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出基于反向传播优化的SLM相位剖面设计，实现信息与能量双重高效的空间模式解复用(SPADE)量子测量，用于两个非相干点源的质心-间距估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息型SPADE成本低、光路稳定、易实现，广泛用于原理验证实验，但光子利用率低，严重制约其实际应用。

Method: 利用交替傅里叶变换与相位调制可实现酉光学变换的思路，通过反向传播优化空间光调制器(SLM)的相位剖面以设计量子测量；以Fisher信息或辅助神经网络估计器的估计误差作为优化目标。

Conclusion: 该方案在信息与能量效率上均具优势，展现出超分辨成像的应用潜力。

Abstract: Spatial-mode demultiplexing (SPADE) has made significant progress in incoherent optical imaging and has been implemented through various experimental approaches. Holography-based SPADE is widely used in proof-of-principle experiments because of its low cost, stable optical arrangement, and ease of implementation. However, its low photon utilization severely limits its practical applications. Alternating Fourier transforms and phase modulations can implement unitary optical transformations. Building on this idea, we use backpropagation to optimize the phase profiles of spatial light modulators (SLMs) and thereby design quantum measurements that are efficient in both information and energy. We study centroid--separation estimation for two incoherent point sources, using Fisher information or the estimation errors of an auxiliary neural network estimator as optimization objectives. As the number of SLMs increases, the optimized measurements approach the quantum limits characterized by the quantum Cramér-Rao bound and the information-regret tradeoff relation. With a few SLMs, our method can outperform direct imaging, showing its potential for superresolution imaging.

</details>

*André T. Cesário,Marcela Herrera*

TL;DR: 本文为能隙随谱控制参量反比变化（Δ(V)=Δ₀/V）的量子比特建立有效热力学，完整给出Helmholtz势、状态方程U=PV、第一定律与Maxwell关系，并分析Carnot与Otto循环效率及Otto不可逆性的熵产来源，最后澄清“超Carnot”表观效率的成因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子热力学中常出现能隙依赖“体积样”控制参量的情形，但缺乏与之自洽的完整热力学框架；同时，量子循环（尤其Otto）的不可逆性与表观超Carnot效率的物理来源需要精确刻画。

Method: 从正则Gibbs态出发，以Helmholtz势及其Legendre变换与稳定性性质构建热力学结构，推导S(U,V)、广义压强、状态方程U=PV、第一定律和Maxwell关系；分析可逆绝热过程（Gibbs布居不变，TV=const.、PV²=const.，即γ=2），比较Carnot与Otto循环；用绝热输运态与储库Gibbs态之间的量子相对熵之和量化Otto循环的熵产；并通过完整功核算检验非平衡电池的效率界。

Result: 可逆绝热对应γ=2；有效Carnot循环达到η_Carnot=1−T_C/T_H，而Otto循环η_Otto=1−V_min/V_max，在有限功输出下严格低于Carnot；Otto的熵产完全发生在等容热化阶段，等于相应量子相对熵之和；幺正产生的非被动性在忽略制备代价时可表现为超Carnot效率，而计入全部功后热力学上限恢复。

Conclusion: 能隙反比于谱控制参量的量子比特具有自洽的有效热力学结构；Otto不可逆性可归因于等容热化中的信息失配；表观超Carnot效率源于未计入的制备成本，完整功核算下热力学第二定律不被违反。

Abstract: We develop an effective thermodynamics for a qubit whose energy gap depends inversely on a volume-like control parameter, $Δ(V)=Δ_0/V$, where $V$ is a spectral-control coordinate rather than a literal mechanical volume. Starting from the canonical Gibbs state, we derive a complete thermodynamic structure from the Helmholtz potential and its Legendre and stability properties, including the fundamental relation $S(U,V)$, a generalized pressure conjugate to $V$, the equation of state $U=PV$, the first law, and the associated Maxwell relations. Reversible adiabats preserve the Gibbs populations and satisfy $TV=\mathrm{const.}$ and $PV^2=\mathrm{const.}$, corresponding to an effective exponent $γ=2$. The resulting effective Carnot cycle attains the Carnot efficiency, $η_{\mathrm{Carnot}}=1-T_C/T_H$, whereas the Otto cycle yields $η_{\mathrm{Otto}}=1-V_{\min}/V_{\max}$ and remains strictly sub-Carnot at finite work output. We identify the source of Otto irreversibility exactly: entropy is produced entirely during the isochoric thermalizations, and the cycle entropy production quantifies the informational mismatch between the adiabatically transported states and the reservoir-selected Gibbs states through the sum of the corresponding quantum relative entropies. Finally, we show that unitary-generated nonpassivity can act as a nonequilibrium battery, producing an apparent efficiency above Carnot when its preparation cost is omitted. At the same time, complete work accounting restores the thermodynamic bound.

</details>


### [77] [Exponential mixing time for the Davies dynamics of the quantum random energy model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08064)
*Giacomo De Palma,Emanuele Mingione*

TL;DR: 该文证明量子随机能量模型（QREM）在Davies动力学下的热化呈指数级缓慢：当逆温β>β_c且横场0≤Γ<Γ_c(β)时，混合时间几乎必然随系统规模指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QREM的平衡态低能结构（冻结、局域化）此前主要在静态/谱层面被理解，缺乏开系统动力学的严格刻画。作者希望给出自旋玻璃相中"冻结"的动力学表现，说明热化在指数长时间尺度上失效。

Method: 研究H=ΓT+U的n比特哈密顿量，U为构型独立的方差等于系统尺寸的高斯能量，T为单比特Pauli X横向场。采用与单比特Pauli系统—浴耦合相联系的耗散Davies动力学。论证依赖：(1) Gibbs权重由稀疏、彼此相距广泛Hamming距离的深经典构型承载；(2) 借用Manai–Warzel的QREM局域化定理把该几何转移到低能量子本征态；(3) 中间谱扇区的Gibbs权重指数小、单比特浴算符在瓶颈两侧的矩阵元指数小；(4) Davies专属的流量（flux）估计将两点结合得到混合时间的指数下界。结果对所有满足细致平衡与多项式上界的Davies速率一致成立，且不假设本原性。

Result: 对任意β>β_c=√(2log2)与0≤Γ<Γ_c(β)（由e^{ββ_c}=2cosh(βΓ_c(β))定义），混合时间几乎必然以系统规模指数增长；加入标准零Bohr频率退相干项后界不变；结论在无需本原性的宽泛Davies速率类上一致成立。

Conclusion: 在量子转变线以下的整个平衡自旋玻璃相中，QREM表现出指数缓慢热化，给出其冻结、低能局域结构的严格开系统动力学体现。

Abstract: We prove exponentially slow thermalization for the quantum random energy model (QREM) under Davies dynamics. The QREM is the $n$-qubit Hamiltonian $H=ΓT+U$, where $U$ assigns independent Gaussian energies with variance equal to the system size to computational-basis configurations and $T$ is the transverse field generated by single-qubit Pauli $X$ operators. Let $β_c=\sqrt{2\log 2}$ be the freezing inverse temperature of the classical random energy model. For every inverse temperature $β>β_c$ and every transverse-field strength $0\leqΓ<Γ_c(β)$, where $e^{ββ_c}=2\cosh(βΓ_c(β))$, we show that the mixing time of the dissipative Davies dynamics associated with single-qubit Pauli system--bath couplings grows exponentially with the system size, almost surely; the bound is unchanged if the standard zero-Bohr-frequency dephasing terms are included. The result is uniform over all Davies rates satisfying detailed balance and a polynomial upper bound and does not assume primitivity.
  In the spin-glass phase, the Gibbs weight is carried by a sparse family of deep classical configurations that are separated by an extensive Hamming distance. A localization theorem for the QREM [Manai, Warzel, Comm. Math. Phys. 402, 1259 (2023)] transfers this geometry to the corresponding low-energy quantum eigenstates. Consequently, the Gibbs weight of the intermediate spectral sector is exponentially small, while single-qubit bath operators have exponentially small matrix elements between the two macroscopic sides of the bottleneck. A Davies-specific flux estimate then converts these two facts into an exponential lower bound on the mixing time. This gives a rigorous open-system dynamical manifestation of the frozen, localized low-energy structure of the QREM throughout its equilibrium spin-glass phase below the quantum transition line.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机数在科学模拟与密码学中至关重要，随机性与安全性是RNG的核心要求。但经典RNG依赖数学假设，存在安全隐患；量子RNG（QRNG）因缺乏专用单光子源而导致输出非均匀、需重衰减与后处理。

Method: 构建QRNG模型，使用标准相干光子源；在假定的光子到达时间模型下解析地均衡各输出区间的概率（改进分箱方案），以消除分箱偏置，减少对重衰减和后处理的依赖。用真实光子探测时间戳数据进行验证。

Result: 在NIST、Dieharder和ENT测试套件下，将所提分箱方案与传统均匀时间分箱进行统计性能对比，结果表明在测试条件下所提方案具有更优的统计性能。

Conclusion: 解析概率均衡的分箱方法可在标准相干光源下直接获得近似无偏的随机数输出，降低对重衰减与后处理的需求；残余经典噪声与器件不完美仍可能需轻量后处理加以抑制。

Abstract: Random numbers play a vital role in scientific simulations and cryptography. Both randomness and security are critical requirements for random number generators (RNGs). However, classical RNGs often rely on mathematical assumptions that can compromise security, while quantum RNGs (QRNGs) face challenges, including non-uniform output due to the lack of specialized single-photon sources. In this work, we propose a QRNG model that generates uniformly distributed random numbers using a standard coherent photon source, without requiring heavy attenuation or post-processing. Although the raw data is theoretically free of bias, post-processing, if any, may be required to suppress any classical noise and device imperfections. The proposed method analytically equalizes the output probabilities under the assumed photon-arrival-time model, potentially reducing the need for heavy attenuation and post-processing to correct binning bias. We evaluate the method using real photon-detection timestamps and compare its statistical performance with conventional uniform time binning using the NIST, Dieharder, and ENT test suites. The results demonstrate improved statistical performance for the proposed binning scheme under the tested conditions.

</details>


### [79] [Quantum trajectory of a spin oscillator, continuous retrodiction beyond the Heisenberg uncertainty relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08104)
*Peiying Li,Junlei Duan,Suotang Jia,Klaus Mølmer,Yanhong Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用5.17×10^10个铷原子构成的spin oscillator，通过弱连续测量并结合时刻t前后的信息，实验实现对非对易自旋可观测量过去测量结果的反演猜测，其方差在1.4小时内持续低于海森堡不确定关系所设界限约1.01 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海森堡不确定关系限制了对任意非对易可观测量未来测量结果的预测精度，但这种限制并不适用于对过去测量结果的反演猜测（retrodiction）。作者希望实验验证这一理论可能性。

Method: 构建由5.17×10^10个铷原子组成的spin oscillator；对满足[x̂_A, p̂_A]=iℏ的共轭自旋可观测量进行弱连续测量；融合时刻t之前与之后获得的信息，对t时刻x̂_A与p̂_A的测量结果进行反演估计，并推广至任意quadrature。

Result: 反演得到的两个共轭量方差在1.4小时内同时低于海森堡不确定关系界限约1.01 dB；对任意quadrature的测量结果也能以相同精度反演。

Conclusion: 实验证明非对易可观测量的过去测量结果可被反演至超越海森堡界限的精度，对量子测量基础研究及结合轨迹策略与纠缠策略的量子增强传感具有前景。

Abstract: The Heisenberg uncertainty relation in quantum mechanics limits the precision of predicting the outcome of measurements of any non-commuting observables. However, this restriction does not apply to retrodictive guessing of the outcomes of past measurements. Here, we experimentally demonstrate this using a spin oscillator consisting of $ 5.17 \times 10^{10}$ rubidium atoms. Through weak continuous measurements, we monitor canonical spin observables $\hat{x}_{\mathrm{A}}$ and $\hat{p}_{\mathrm{A}}$ with $[\hat{x}_{\mathrm{A}},\hat{p}_{\mathrm{A}}] = i\hbar$. By combining information obtained before and after time $t$, we retrodict the outcomes of measurements of $\hat{x}_{\mathrm{A}}$ and $\hat{p}_{\mathrm{A}}$ at time $t$ with variances that remain, concurrently over 1.4 hours, $\sim$ 1.01 dB below the bound set by the Heisenberg uncertainty relation. We also infer and demonstrate the ability to retrodict, with the same precision, the measurement outcomes for arbitrary quadratures of the spin oscillator. This brings interesting prospects for further research on quantum measurements, and for quantum-enhanced sensing that combines trajectory and entanglement strategies.

</details>


### [80] [Semi-Analytical Geometric Synthesis of Robust Quantum Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08107)
*Eduardo Willwock Lussi,Rafael de Santiago,Eduardo Inacio Duzzioni,Hector Bessa Silveira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于空间曲线量子控制(SCQC) formalism 的半解析自动化方法，用 Frenet-Serret 框架参数化曲线、以 Pontryagin 极大值原理(PMP)构造边值问题并用打靶法求解，可数十毫秒内合成对振幅与退相位误差鲁棒的平滑动态纠错门(DCG)，2,000个单比特旋转平均局部优化时间 39.945 ms，名义门不保真度低于 10^-9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真量子门是容错量子计算的核心需求，但受量子比特对环境噪声的敏感性与控制非理想性限制。动态纠错门(DCG)可抑制误差影响，然而现有合成方法多依赖数值优化，单门合成时间达分钟量级，成本过高，亟需大幅降低合成开销且保持保真度与鲁棒性。

Method: 基于 Space Curve Quantum Control (SCQC) 形式化：将量子演化映射为几何空间曲线，由其几何性质决定目标门与鲁棒性。在 Frenet-Serret 框架下参数化曲线（用 Rodrigues 公式积分），采用 Pontryagin 极大值原理(PMP)设计同时抗振幅误差与退相位误差的控制，将 PMP 表述为边值问题并以打靶法求解。以参考解为起点逐步改变目标旋转角构建解库，通过插值为新目标门的打靶参数提供初值，实现自动化半解析合成。

Result: 在 2,000 个单比特旋转上评估：插值基准备完成后，平均局部优化时间为 39.945 ms/门；经虚拟相位校正后名义门不保真度低于 10^-9；并验证了对准静态振幅误差与退相位误差的鲁棒性。保真度与鲁棒性与数值优化方法相当，而合成成本降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法将 DCG 合成从分钟级数值优化降至数十毫秒级的半解析自动化流程，兼顾高保真与鲁棒性，为可扩展的容错量子计算门综合提供高效工具；其有效性在单比特旋转上得到验证，解库插值策略是加速新目标门合成的关键。

Abstract: High-fidelity quantum gates are a central requirement for fault-tolerant quantum computation, yet their implementation is limited by the sensitivity of qubits to environmental noise and by control non-idealities. To mitigate these effects, quantum gates can be designed as dynamically corrected gates (DCGs), where control sequences are engineered to suppress the impact of errors on the resulting operation. Many methods in the literature are able to synthesize DCGs, but they typically rely on numerical optimization, with synthesis times on the order of minutes per gate. Here, we propose an automated semi-analytical method that reduces this cost by orders of magnitude, synthesizing smooth, robust control pulses in tens of milliseconds rather than minutes, with comparable fidelity and robustness. Our approach is based on the Space Curve Quantum Control (SCQC) formalism, where quantum evolution is mapped onto a geometric space curve whose geometric properties determine the target gate and its robustness. We parameterize the curve in the Frenet-Serret frame and employ the Pontryagin Maximum Principle (PMP) to design controls that are robust against both amplitude and dephasing errors. The PMP is formulated as a boundary-value problem and solved using a shooting method. The Frenet-Serret frame is integrated using Rodrigues' formula. Starting from a reference solution, we gradually vary the target rotation angle to construct a library of solutions, whose interpolation provides initial guesses for the shooting parameters of new target gates. We evaluated the method on 2,000 single-qubit rotations, obtaining a mean local optimization time of 39.945 ms per gate after preparation of the interpolation base. Quantum propagation yielded nominal gate infidelities below 10^-9 after virtual phase correction, with verified robustness against quasistatic amplitude and dephasing errors.

</details>


### [81] [Accelerating Quantum-Classical Auxiliary-Field Quantum Monte Carlo with Shadow-Trained Neural Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08129)
*Justin Provazza,Oliver Knitter,Joshua Goings,Evgeny Epifanovsky,Chi Chen,Martin Roetteler,Luning Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将matchgate shadow数据蒸馏为对称性约束、噪声感知的自回归Transformer神经量子态（NQS），用作AFQMC试探态，避免对shadow快照反复求和，显著降低经典计算开销并提升噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QC-AFQMC用matchgate shadows表示试探态，每次计算试探-行走子重叠与局域能量都需对大量快照求平均，成为经典计算瓶颈；同时硬件噪声导致标准QC-AFQMC结果定性错误。

Method: 构建对称性约束、噪声感知、自回归的Transformer神经量子态，从matchgate shadow数据中蒸馏学习；训练完成后，NQS直接给出重叠、力偏置与局域能量，无需再对shadow求和。

Result: 在镍催化氧化加成反应中，噪声感知NQS在统计误差内复现理想upCCD-AFQMC的势垒与反应能；而使用同一shadow数据的标准QC-AFQMC因硬件噪声结果定性失准；镍体系经典成本相比此前实现降低约99%。

Conclusion: 以NQS替代shadow快照平均，可在保持精度的同时大幅降低经典开销并增强噪声鲁棒性，为QC-AFQMC的实用化提供可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum-classical auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo (QC-AFQMC) uses a trial state prepared on a quantum computer to improve the accuracy of the classical AFQMC algorithm. State-of-the-art QC-AFQMC represents this state with matchgate shadows, so every trial-walker overlap and local energy must be averaged over many snapshots, making this classical computation a bottleneck. Here we distill the matchgate shadow data into a symmetry-constrained, noise-aware, autoregressive transformer neural quantum state (NQS). Used as the AFQMC trial state, the NQS evaluates overlaps, force biases, and local energies without repeatedly summing over the matchgate shadows. For a nickel-catalyzed oxidative-addition reaction, the noise-awareness of our NQS design successfully recovers the ideal-upCCD AFQMC barrier and reaction energy within statistical uncertainty, while standard QC-AFQMC execution with the same shadow data yields qualitatively inaccurate results as a result of hardware noise. Since the shadow data are needed only for training, the estimated classical cost for the nickel system falls by approximately 99% relative to our previous QC-AFQMC implementation.

</details>

Abstract: Simulating strongly correlated electrons in molecules and materials remains a central goal for quantum computing. This accelerates quantum-classical QMC by orders of magnitude, enabling chemically accurate simulations on up to 40 qubits, more than doubling the scale of previous hardware demonstrations with comparable accuracy. We demonstrate our approach on hydrogen chains and strongly correlated iron-sulfur clusters using measurements from Google's Willow processor. By enabling efficient reuse of quantum-state information on the classical coprocessor, our framework addresses a fundamental bottleneck in hybrid quantum algorithms that will persist even into the fault-tolerant era.

Abstract: We present a generalization of the out of time order correlator (OTOC) for non-unitary systems. Utilizing transfer matrix machinery, we find that the OTOC asymptotically decays exponentially and that the decay depends on the rate of measurement. In the thermodynamic limit, with sparse measurement, the OTOC approximately exhibits a Gaussian shape. Phenomenologically, this corresponds to the development of a causal scrambling front where unlike the unitary case, the signal decays in the bulk of the causal cone. We develop a Monte Carlo framework to compute the OTOC. We then estimate the OTOC directly via quantum simulation and on Quantinuum's trapped-ion quantum computer. We resolve the unitary and non-unitary OTOC phenomenology despite the presence of hardware noise. Our analytic results characterize how local nonunitary channels affect OTOC decay, providing a quantitative benchmark for the cumulative effects of a broad class of hardware noise on many-body dynamics.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 相对化于经典 oracle，考虑 Bravyi–Bessen–Terhal 提出的 StoqMA：验证者仅做经典可逆计算，最后进行一次 Hadamard 基测量。构造的分离问题为：给定邻接表 oracle 描述的稀疏高围长图，判定其 Perron–Frobenius 本征值是大还是小，即某类 stoquastic Hamiltonian 的基态能量估计任务。构造继承图探索问题中指数级量子加速的线索——从 welded trees（STOC 2003）到近期构造（STOC 2021、arXiv 2026）。

Result: 得到 MA ⊊ StoqMA 的相对化分离。这说明 StoqMA（与无符号问题的 stoquastic Hamiltonian 复杂度紧密相关、看似更易处理的类别）在 oracle 设定下严格强于 MA。

Conclusion: 极小的量子资源（单次 Hadamard 基测量）已足以超越任意经典验证者；该结果将量子优势的来源定位到 stoquastic 量子计算层面，并把量子验证分离的链条向下延伸至 MA 与 StoqMA 之间。

Abstract: Several landmark oracle results (FOCS 2000, FOCS 2025, STOC 2026) establish strict separations along the chain $\mathsf{MA} \subseteq \mathsf{QCMA} \subseteq \mathsf{QMA}$, showing that quantum verification and quantum witnesses can each provide additional power over classical proof systems. These separations, however, use substantial coherent quantum computation, often through the quantum Fourier transform. We prove that a minimal quantum resource already suffices to outperform every classical verifier. Namely, we show the separation $$\mathsf{MA} \subsetneq \mathsf{StoqMA}$$ relative to a classical oracle. The class $\mathsf{StoqMA}$, introduced by Bravyi, Bessen, and Terhal (arXiv 2006), restricts the verifier to classical reversible computation, followed by a single Hadamard-basis measurement. Its power is closely tied to the complexity of stoquastic Hamiltonians, a sign-problem-free and seemingly more tractable class of quantum Hamiltonians.
  Our separating problem is to determine whether a sparse high-girth graph, given via an adjacency-list oracle, has a large or small Perron--Frobenius eigenvalue. This amounts to a ground-energy estimation task for a related stoquastic Hamiltonian. Our construction builds on a line of work on graph exploration problems with exponential quantum speedups, from welded trees (STOC 2003) to recent constructions (STOC 2021, arXiv 2026).

</details>

Motivation: 量子软件栈包含量子编程语言、优化编译器、量子模拟器和硬件控制器，但相比传统编译器与架构模拟器，由于结果具有概率性、缺乏明确硬件规范、量子编程复杂度高，QSS 难以测试。

Method: QDIFF 差分测试方法，包含三项创新：(1) 通过保语义的源到源变换生成待测输入程序以探索程序变体；(2) 分析电路深度、双量子门操作、门错误率、T1弛豫时间等静态特征，过滤掉不值得在昂贵量子硬件上执行的电路以加速差分测试；(3) 基于 Kolmogorov–Smirnov 检验与交叉熵等分布比较函数，设计可扩展的等价性检查机制。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数据与量化结论，主要贡献为方法框架设计。

Conclusion: QDIFF 为难以测试的量子软件栈提供了一套可扩展的差分测试方案，兼顾测试程序生成的多样性与昂贵硬件执行的效率。

Abstract: Over the past few years, several quantum software stacks (QSS) have been developed in response to rapid hardware advances in quantum computing. A QSS includes a quantum programming language, an optimizing compiler that translates a quantum algorithm written in a high-level language into quantum gate instructions, a quantum simulator that emulates these instructions on a classical device, and a software controller that sends analog signals to a very expensive quantum hardware based on quantum circuits. In comparison to traditional compilers and architecture simulators, QSSes are difficult to tests due to the probabilistic nature of results, the lack of clear hardware specifications, and quantum programming complexity.
  This work devises a novel differential testing approach for QSSes, named QDIFF with three major innovations: (1) We generate input programs to be tested via semantics-preserving, source to source transformation to explore program variants. (2) We speed up differential testing by filtering out quantum circuits that are not worthwhile to execute on quantum hardware by analyzing static characteristics such as a circuit depth, 2 qubit gate operations, gate error rates, and T1 relaxation time. (3) We design an extensible equivalence checking mechanism via distribution comparison functions such as Kolmogorov Smirnov test and cross entropy

</details>
