Abstract: For unknown three-qubit pure states, we characterize the optimal trade-off among GHZ-class certification, binary probability readout of the three-tangle, and conditional state retention. The vanishing ideal of the null class specifies the optimal certification subspace, while a polynomial multiplication operator gives the spectral limit for binary readout. Four copies are necessary for a nonzero certificate. A fifth copy raises the optimal success probability from $τ^{2}/48$ to $5τ^{2}/72$ and permits the original unknown state to be retained, where $τ$ is the three-tangle. We obtain this gain as an instance of a general symmetry-based additional-copy theorem. At six copies the optimal certification probability is $(39+2S)τ^{2}/296$, with $S=\sum_{X}\mathrm{Tr}\,ρ_{X}^{2}$, whereas the largest exact binary probability is $τ^{2}/8$. Local Schur measurements and conditional local operations with classical communication attain these bounds. Retaining the full six-copy branch record yields a weighted estimator with lower variance than binary thinning of the same data. Explicit circuits and fixed-budget comparisons connect the success probabilities to input-copy consumption, retained quantum output, and coherent gate cost.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We calculate the level-resolved transmon population remaining after dispersive readout with a converged open-system model. The drive and qubit-cavity coupling use the rotating-wave approximation, while the cavity is a quantized mode in a frame displaced by its classical trajectory. At a readout occupation of 80, the model leaves a persistent distribution across the first transmon levels, with p2 = 1.02 x 10^-3 and p3 = 4.10 x 10^-5 at the end of readout. These populations survive an additional drive-off wait: over 73 ns each level decays by less than 3 percent and follows its own relaxation rate, not the near-factor-of-two drop in residual cavity photons. This identifies the population as bare-level occupation rather than coherent dressing. Channel-removal and bath-model controls attribute p2 mainly to dephasing and p3 and above mainly to relaxation, unchanged across bath models though absolute values vary by up to 15 percent. We also test the coherent drive truncation. In dissipation-free transmon-only benchmarks driven by the classical cavity trajectory, the nearest-neighbour RWA drive underestimates the full charge-operator result while the drive is on. At switch-off for occupation 80, where coherent populations are hundreds of times larger than the residual values, p2(RWA)/p2(full) = 0.905 and p3(RWA)/p3(full) = 0.759, with larger discrepancies at deeper levels. One source is a parity-allowed non-nearest-neighbour charge matrix element omitted by the drive operator. A naive beyond-RWA extension with bare-basis Lindblad dissipators fails a zero-drive ground-state test and serves only as a diagnostic. A dressed-basis (secular) construction instead leaves the dressed ground state stationary at zero drive. The result is a converged RWA prediction of readout-induced population plus direct tests of where the approximation loses quantitative reliability.

</details>

Abstract: The Gibbs state determined by a thermal reservoir does not determine how a working medium approaches it or how fluctuations accumulate over repeated cycles. We exploit this kinetic freedom in an anharmonic collective quantum Otto engine. A longitudinal interaction separates neighboring Dicke transition frequencies without changing their collective matrix elements, allowing passive spectral shaping to route finite-time transport among edges with different heat costs while preserving the contact Gibbs states. Exact edge-current identities and repeated-cycle counting statistics link this routing to mean work, hot heat, and intercycle work correlations. At fixed thermal-contact exposure, structured reservoirs can increase both mean work and long-time squared precision; equal-work controls distinguish these gains from uniform relaxation-rate changes. The useful regime is mesoscopic, bounded by spectral resolution and physical contact time. An equal-cooling example and a full-spin local-noise study test the mechanism beyond ideal engine operation, the latter retaining a one-cycle precision gain despite spin-sector mixing. In a six-transmon dressed-spectrum model, passive microwave filters at fixed integrated coupling achieve equal mean work with shorter thermal contacts, corresponding to higher contact-time-normalized output, and with long-time squared precision comparable to that of the fastest sampled flat reference. The transition pathways selected by a passive reservoir thus provide a control variable for finite-time performance and accumulated work fluctuations that is independent of thermal equilibrium.

</details>


### [89] [Certifying quantum code and circuit distances at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08247)
*Bin Cheng,Naixu Guo,Feng Pan*

TL;DR: 本文将码距与电路距表达为热带配分函数，结合秩基动态规划、权重界定搜索、电路约简与松弛，并用匹配权重的显式逻辑错误下界完成认证；在两基准上认证的实例数超过所有其他精确求解器之和，最高处理31,200量子比特的码与8×10^5故障机制的存储电路，并审计出26篇近期论文中10篇存在高估距离（最大偏差≥8）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 码距与电路距决定量子纠错的逻辑错误抑制强度，是码比较、调度选择与资源估计的核心指标；但已发表的距离多为启发式搜索给出的上界，缺乏严格认证，可能误导设计决策。

Method: 将两种距离写成热带配分函数，用秩基动态规划与权重受限搜索求值；通过电路约简与松弛缩减问题规模；用与求得权重相同的显式逻辑错误作为可证明下界，与上界相接以认证精确值。

Result: 在两套基准上认证实例数超过所有对照精确求解器之和；认证规模达31,200量子比特（如Google模块化双曲码）与8×10^5故障机制（高速率Cornucopia码存储电路）。审计26篇近期论文，10篇存在高估距离或非紧上界，最大偏差至少为8。IBM双gross存储电路在标准调度下认证电路距为16（而非假设的18），重设计调度可在一噪声周期下达到18，代价是每周期多一个时间步。在99.98%结良率下，保留认证距离≥10的含死校验gross码存储块，可将可用比例从仅用完美块的53%提升至94%。

Conclusion: 严格认证的距离可纠正文献中的上界偏差，并直接指导电路与调度设计、评估缺陷芯片可用性，具有实际工程价值。

Abstract: Code and circuit distances characterize how strongly quantum error correction suppresses logical errors and guide code comparisons, schedule choices and resource estimates. Yet many published distances are only upper bounds from heuristic searches. Here, we express both distances as tropical partition functions and evaluate them with rank-based dynamic programming and weight-bounded search. Circuit reductions and relaxations shrink the problems, and proved lower bounds with explicit logical errors of matching weight certify exact values. On two benchmarks, our method certifies more instances than all other tested exact solvers combined. We certify distances for codes with up to 31,200 qubits, such as Google's modular hyperbolic codes, and for memory circuits with up to $8\times10^5$ fault mechanisms, such as those of high-rate Cornucopia codes. Our audit of 26 recent papers identifies overestimated distances and non-tight upper bounds in 10 papers, with a maximum discrepancy of at least eight. Certified distances also guide circuit design and show which defective chips remain usable. In particular, we certify circuit distance 16 for IBM's two-gross memory circuits with the standard schedule, not the assumed 18, whereas redesigned schedules reach 18 at one noisy cycle, at the cost of one more timestep per cycle. At 99.98% junction yield, keeping gross-code memory blocks with dead checks that are certified at distance 10 or more raises the estimated usable share from 53% for perfect blocks alone to 94% with an improved schedule.

</details>

*Roberto Rubboli,Bartosz Regula,Mario Berta,Ludovico Lami,Marco Tomamichel*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵自Clausius提出以来在物理学中作用关键，Shannon将其引入信息论；如今信息论与物理的交互表明，单一熵概念无法刻画物理系统的全部相关性质，因此需要多种具有不同特性的量子信息熵度量。

Method: 以结构化方式综述近期进展：全面覆盖各类信息度量（含Rényi熵、平滑熵及其相互关系），并从自然物理原理出发对熵进行系统化刻画以论证其定义的合理性。

Conclusion: 不存在单一的熵概念足以涵盖物理系统的所有相关性质，需采用多种具有不同特征与用途的熵度量；该综述为理解与选用这些度量提供了统一的系统性框架。

Abstract: Entropy has played a pivotal role in physics ever since its inception by Clausius in the early industrial age. Shannon ushered the concept into the information age by using it to describe fundamental limits of information processing, founding the field of information theory. Today, a fruitful interaction between information theory and physics is pushing our understanding of entropy forward. It has revealed, in particular, that no single notion of entropy suffices to capture all relevant properties of physical systems, making it necessary to consider a variety of entropic measures of quantum information, each with distinct features and uses. This review surveys these recent advances in a structured way, covering a comprehensive array of information measures, including Rényi and smooth entropies, together with their interrelations. Starting with a systematic characterization of entropies that motivates their definitions from natural physical principles, we discuss their applications ranging from quantum communication and cryptography to resource theories, thermodynamics, and many-body physics.

</details>

*Angela Sara Cacciapuoti,Jessica Illiano,Caterina De Risi,Marcello Caleffi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出"量子原生网络功能"视角：量子现象不仅作为被传输的资源，还可直接参与网络功能的实现。

Method: 采用Perspective式论证：先以纠缠作为"有状态网络资源"建立架构基线（状态非局域分布、可被操作与消耗、因退相干而退化，且状态须对作用于其上的功能可见）；再以两个机制作为量子原生功能设计空间的例证——相干空间路径叠加下的传播中纠缠生成，以及共享纠缠诱导的、可偏离物理图拓扑的操作连通性；据此提出"量子寻址"作为横切抽象。

Result: 论证路径、邻居、参与节点等的操作相关性可由量子相干与共享纠缠决定，而非仅由物理连通性决定；量子寻址可表征此类关系，且补充而非替代经典寻址与信令。

Conclusion: 量子互联网由此从"传输量子信息与分发纠缠"拓展为"在网络运行中利用量子现象"，开辟涵盖路由、转发、控制与功能部署的耦合前沿。

Abstract: The Quantum Internet is commonly envisioned as a network that transports quantum states and distributes entanglement as the key resource underpinning distributed quantum applications. This view is necessary but incomplete if quantum phenomena are confined to what the network carries or manages, while network functionalities remain grounded predominantly in classical abstractions. This Perspective advances a broader thesis: quantum phenomena can also participate directly in the realization of network functionalities. We first establish the architectural baseline by introducing entanglement as a stateful network resource whose operational condition is non-locally distributed across remote nodes, evolves through manipulation or consumption, and degrades over time through decoherence. Its evolving state must therefore remain visible to the functionalities acting on it. We then move beyond resource visibility and use two mechanisms as proof points of a broader quantum-native functional design space: entanglement generation during propagation through coherent spatial-path superposition, and operational connectivity induced by shared entanglement that can depart from the physical graph. These examples show that paths, neighbors, or participating nodes may become operationally relevant in ways determined by quantum coherence and shared entanglement, rather than by physical connectivity alone. We identify Quantum Addressing as a transversal abstraction capable of representing such relations while complementing, rather than replacing, classical addressing and signaling. The resulting perspective broadens the Quantum Internet from transporting quantum information and distributing entanglement to exploiting quantum phenomena in network functioning, opening a coupled frontier spanning routing, forwarding, control, and functionality placement.

</details>

*Long Huang*

TL;DR: 在固定测量基下，穷举n=3,5,7量子比特重复码的恢复方案，给出最小单比特Pauli门数0、1、5，远低于标准最小权解码的6、10、14；门数只取决于纠错位点数目而非具体位置，且三比特门设计在任何工作点都严格更精确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于稳定子码的过程层析通常把译码器固定为标准最小权解码，而在固定测量基下解码器所用单比特Pauli门本质是一种可计量资源；作者希望回答"译码器真正需要多少个物理Pauli门"，并厘清门数与精度、秩之间的真实关系。

Method: 对n=3、5、7的重复码穷举所有恢复（recovery）设计，用两个素数上的精确模运算判定信道矩阵的秩，统计所需单比特Pauli门数；与一阶解码和完整最小权解码比较，并在校准残差底噪下评估精度曲线与镜像对（链反演协变性）问题。

Result: 最小门数分别为0、1、5，对比一阶解码6、10、14与完整最小权解码6、50、308。门数仅由被纠正位点数决定，与位置无关，故设计问题的两半可分离。纠错更多位点并非单调更优：n=7时完整解码损失3个秩维度；在校准残差下n=3精度曲线呈U形。三比特门设计在40个采样工作点中位数上精度提升9.5倍，且每一点都更优（1.8–88倍）。在良态工作点达到10 mrad rms误差需1.2×10^7次测量、约65 s；n≥5受限于条件数而非门数。最优设计不具链反演协变性，对镜像对给出1.3×10^-3的区分而Jacobian仍满秩。

Conclusion: 解码器门数可作为资源被显著压缩（n=7时由14降至5），门数与精度不能由"纠错位点越多越好"的直觉替代；大n的瓶颈是条件数而非门数；最优设计破坏镜像对称，可作为区分链反演简并的判据。

Abstract: Process tomography built on stabilizer codes compares the logical channel of a repetition code with a reference channel, and the decoder that produces that channel is almost always fixed to the standard minimum-weight one. We ask how many physical single-qubit Pauli gates the decoder actually needs when the measurement basis is fixed---the constraint under which the count is a resource rather than a bookkeeping artefact. Exhaustive enumeration of every recovery design for $n=3$, $5$ and $7$ qubits, with rank decided by exact modular arithmetic at two primes, gives minimal counts of $0$, $1$ and $5$ gates, against $6$, $10$ and $14$ for first-order decoding and $6$, $50$ and $308$ for full minimum-weight decoding. The count depends only on how many sites are corrected and never on which, so the two halves of the design problem separate. Correcting more sites is not monotonically better: at $n=7$ full decoding loses three dimensions of rank, and under a calibration-residual floor the precision curve at $n=3$ is U-shaped. The three-gate design is $9.5$ times more precise than the standard six-gate decoder at the median of $40$ sampled working points, and more precise at every one of them ($1.8$ to $88$ times); the size of that factor belongs to the working point, the ordering does not. Reaching a $10$\,mrad rms error then costs $1.2\times10^{7}$ shots in $65$\,s on a current superconducting device at a well-conditioned point, while $n\ge5$ stays out of reach for conditioning reasons rather than gate-count reasons. Finally, the optimal designs are not covariant under chain reversal: they separate the mirror pair of arXiv:2610.19779 by $1.3\times10^{-3}$ while leaving the Jacobian at full rank.

</details>

Abstract: We present an experimental proposal to test whether gravity can entangle a levitated particle with an array of quantum clocks. By harnessing the collective interaction between a micrometer-scale particle and an array of ultra-precise nuclear clocks, our scheme offers a conceivable path to observing gravitationally mediated entanglement with quantum clocks in the laboratory. Crucially, this setup will probe a genuinely relativistic effect one that requires the contribution of internal clock energy to active gravitational mass and cannot arise from the Newtonian interaction between fixed masses. Therefore, observing this entanglement would provide evidence for the quantised nature of active gravitational mass -- which has not been tested in any prior experiment -- rather than for spatial coherence of fixed masses,which is routinely tested in matterwave interferometers

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Erbium-doped solids like $^{167}\mathrm{Er}^{3+}:\mathrm{Y}_2\mathrm{SiO}_5$ have properties that make them suitable for quantum-network memories, however, their unquenched effective electronic spin causes rapid magnetic decoherence. Operating at Zero First-Order Zeeman (ZEFOZ) points allows us to mitigate this, where we evaluate the ZEFOZ landscape for Site-2 $^{167}\mathrm{Er}^{3+}$ by incorporating a full-Hessian spin-Hamiltonian treatment with the anisotropic magnetic-field covariance derived from the native $^{89}\mathrm{Y}$ crystal lattice directly. Operating-point screening criteria were adopted to devise a Pareto-optimal frontier of four transition pairs to balance modeled Y-only magnetic pure-dephasing lifetimes ($T_{φ,Y}^{(2)}$) against microwave control authority. The $ε_{14}\leftrightarrowε_{15}$ transition exhibits the longest modeled Y-bath-induced quadratic pure-dephasing time, $T_{φ,Y}^{(2)}>2.2\times10^5~\mathrm{s}$, while the $ε_{12}\leftrightarrowε_{13}$ transition stood out, achieving a Rabi frequency coefficient of $1.248 \times 10^5~\mathrm{Hz/mT}$. An assumption-based Hamiltonian-uncertainty analysis demonstrates that while uncompensated deviations shift the ZEFOZ condition away from its nominal magnetic-field coordinate, the shifted operating points can be recovered via numerical vector-field recalibration. The Pareto-optimal coordinates were mathematically recovered for all sampled Hamiltonian perturbations, and the $84.1^{\mathrm{st}}$-percentile field relocations ranged from $10.95$ to $27.97~\mathrm{mT}$. The intended operation near $10~\mathrm{mK}$ must be taken into account for the suppression of Er-induced spectral diffusion through electronic-spin polarization, while the extended Y-bath pure-dephasing timescales also remain conditional on the adopted temporal-correlation model.

</details>

Conclusion: 研究为量子极限下空间传感的实用策略提供了更广泛的性能基准，并指出孪生Fock态等可实现的态在横向位移测量中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: We investigate the ultimate sensitivity limits for measuring small transverse beam displacements and identify optimal strategies that attain them, using the framework of mode parameter estimation. We contrast two families of states with different resource constraints, using Hermite-Gauss and Laguerre-Gauss mode pairs. First, for fixed total photon number states, the optimal state is identified and we show that it asymptotically approaches a rotated NOON state. The sensitivity bound is compared to that of an asymptotically optimal pure Gaussian state with fixed average photon number. Finally, we propose concrete measurement schemes using the method of moments and show that the twin-Fock state may provide a viable alternative to the rotated NOON state for this task. Our work provides a broader benchmark for developing practical strategies for spatial sensing at the quantum limit.

</details>


### [96] [DiffSequence-Q: Diffusion Design with Quantum Assistance for Biologically Active Proteins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08434)

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffSequence-Q，一种以残基级二级结构和连续溶解度目标为条件的混合多项式扩散模型，训练时用QNN/QKAN作为溶解度预测辅助头提供量子辅助监督，生成阶段完全采用经典逆向扩散；在CATH上二级结构恢复率最高，预测溶解度分别比MLP基线提高14.0%（QNN）与22.7%（QKAN），并经实验验证三类蛋白家族具生物活性。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同时满足二级结构约束与溶解度目标的蛋白质序列设计仍然困难，需要一种能把结构要求与连续可溶性指标联合纳入生成过程的框架。

Method: 构建条件于每个残基二级结构与连续溶解度目标的混合多项式扩散模型；在共享Transformer表征之上接量子神经网络（QNN）或量子Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（QKAN）作为辅助溶解度预测头，将辅助预测损失与扩散目标联合优化，使量子辅助监督影响去噪模型学习；推理时仅使用经典逆向扩散生成序列。

Abstract: Designing protein sequences that satisfy both secondary structure requirements and solubility targets remains challenging. We present DiffSequence-Q, a hybrid multinomial diffusion model conditioned on secondary structure at each residue and a continuous solubility target. To introduce quantum auxiliary supervision during training, we employ a quantum neural network (QNN) or quantum Kolmogorov-Arnold network (QKAN) as an auxiliary solubility prediction head over shared Transformer representations. The auxiliary prediction loss is jointly optimized with the diffusion objective, allowing quantum auxiliary supervision to influence the learned denoising model. Once trained, protein sequences are generated entirely through classical reverse diffusion. On the CATH benchmark, DiffSequence-Q achieved the highest secondary structure recovery among the four evaluated models for complete sequence generation. Evaluation with a separately trained solubility prediction model showed that the best QNN and QKAN configurations achieved mean predicted solubility scores 14.0% and 22.7% higher than the multilayer perceptron (MLP) baseline, respectively. Experimental characterization further demonstrated biological activity across three distinct protein families. Selected defensins exhibited pronounced inhibitory activity against Aspergillus niger, while xylanases and short-chain dehydrogenases exhibited substantial enzymatic activity. Together, these results support DiffSequence-Q as a framework for integrating structural requirements and solubility targets in protein generation, with experimentally demonstrated biological activity.

</details>


### [97] [Polynomial-time classical simulation of boson and fermion sampling under thermal loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08444)
*Armando Angrisani,Hela Mhiri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立统一框架，证明在热损耗（含纯损耗）下玻色与费米采样的经典可模拟性：对任意固定非零总损耗率，Fock态玻色采样、固定均匀压缩高斯玻色采样及带magic输入的费米采样可在多项式时间内以逆多项式全变差误差采样（对Haar随机干涉仪高概率成立）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样实验是展示量子计算优势的主流途径，但其对实验噪声的鲁棒性尚未被完全理解，需厘清热损耗下量子优势是否仍然成立。

Method: 构建玻色与费米采样在热损耗（含纯损耗）下的统一经典模拟框架；分别处理Fock态玻色采样、固定均匀压缩的高斯玻色采样、带magic输入的费米采样；通过Haar随机干涉仪高概率论证与理想输出分布的"平坦性"假设进行复杂度分析，并分析逐层常数热损耗下的深度依赖。

Result: 任意固定非零总损耗率下，三类采样任务均可多项式时间、以逆多项式全变差误差采样（对Haar随机干涉仪高概率）；较弱的平坦性假设即可在无Haar随机性时实现准多项式时间模拟；逐层常数热损耗配合平坦性假设，在对数深度下实现多项式时间模拟；Fock态玻色采样的界覆盖稀疏与饱和两种情形，且在接近满输入占据时更强——仅有限个输入模式为空时，Haar随机干涉仪下的含噪输出随模式数增长收敛到与干涉仪无关的分布。

Conclusion: 热损耗显著削弱玻色/费米采样的经典难解性：在任何固定非零损耗率下均存在高效经典模拟，量子优势声明需明确损耗低于何种标度，并为实验参数设定提供了理论边界。

Abstract: Sampling experiments are a leading approach to demonstrating quantum computational advantage, yet their robustness to experimental noise is not completely understood. We develop a common framework for classical simulation of boson and fermion sampling under thermal loss, including pure loss. For Fock-state boson sampling, Gaussian boson sampling with fixed uniform squeezing, and fermion sampling with magic inputs, we obtain polynomial-time sampling at inverse-polynomial total variation error, for any fixed nonzero overall loss rate, and with high probability over Haar-random interferometers. A milder flatness assumption on the ideal output distribution suffices for quasipolynomial-time simulation without Haar randomness. With constant thermal loss per layer, the same flatness assumption gives polynomial-time simulation at logarithmic depth. Our bounds for Fock-state boson sampling cover both dilute and saturated regimes and become stronger near full input filling: when only a bounded number of input modes are empty, the noisy output for Haar-random interferometers converges, as the number of modes grows, to a distribution independent of the interferometer.

</details>


### [98] [Room-Temperature Quantum Emission from Boron Nitride Nanotubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08460)
*Ke-Sean J. M. Peter,Ryan A. Kowalski,David A. Curie,Dakota Braaten,Matthew W. Day,Denis R. Candido,Deyu Li,Richard F. Haglund,Joshua D. Caldwell,Richarda Niemann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究氮化硼纳米管（BNNT）作为室温单光子源（SPE）的潜力，发现其可在全可见光波段发射单光子、单管多波长、寿命短至0.5 ns，并通过表面钝化提升稳定性，适合集成到纳米光子器件中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子密钥分发与量子通信需要明亮、高纯度、稳定的单光子源；hBN薄片虽为常用SPE宿主材料，但二维体系存在工程复杂度问题，因此希望借助BNNT进一步降低维度以获得更易集成的SPE。

Method: 采用共聚焦光致发光显微术与Hanbury Brown–Twiss干涉测量，对BNNT作为室温SPE进行表征；并实施表面钝化/包覆以改善稳定性。

Result: 在BNNT中发现覆盖整个可见光谱的量子发射体，包括hBN中尚未报道SPE的谱区；每根管沿长度方向含多个不同波长的SPE，寿命快至0.5 ns（约为最快hBN SPE的两倍）；表面钝化可抑制闪烁、提高稳定性；管径仅约25 nm。

Conclusion: BNNT可作为单一结构承载多波长SPE源，兼具紧凑尺寸（~25 nm），非常适合确定性放置并集成于波导、干涉滤波片等纳米光子器件。

Abstract: Quantum information systems such as quantum key distribution and quantum communications require bright, high-purity, stable sources of single photons to act as carriers of quantum information. As such, methods for obtaining single photon emitters (SPEs) in solid-state devices capable of articulating with quantum photonic circuits are a necessary obstacle that must be overcome. Hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) flakes have been extensively studied as a host material for SPEs, though working in two dimensions is accompanied by engineering complexity. However, dimensionality can be reduced through the implementation of boron nitride nanotubes (BNNTs). Here we investigate the potential of BNNTs as room-temperature SPEs using confocal photoluminescence microscopy and Hanbury Brown-Twiss interferometry. Quantum emitters were found in BNNTs spanning the entirety of the visible spectrum, including spectral regions where SPEs within hBN have not been reported. Furthermore, each tube was found to possess multiple SPEs at varying emission wavelengths along the length, exhibiting lifetimes as fast as 0.5 ns, which is about two-fold faster than the fastest hBN SPEs. Additionally, we demonstrate that surface passivation/coating mitigates blinking events, therefore increasing the emitter stability. Thus, BNNTs can function as a single structure supporting a variety of multiwavelength SPE sources, while maintaining a compact radius of ~25 nm, making them ideally suited for deterministic placement and integration within nanophotonic devices such as waveguides and interferometric filters.

</details>


### [99] [Quantum Knowledge Distillation: State-of-the-art, Applications and Open Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08461)
*Aldana Al-Sulaiti,Kuan-Cheng Chen,Muhammad Bilal Akram Dastagir,Ahmed Farouk,Saif Al-Kuwari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该综述从“师生配置”和“量子资源角色”两条互补分类轴梳理量子机器学习中的知识蒸馏，涵盖量子启发、经典模拟量子计算与量子硬件执行三类方法，并批判性评估其收益、成本与证据强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏在量子机器学习中的应用已从模型压缩扩展到跨架构迁移、电路近似、分布式学习以及用量子监督训练模型的经典部署，但相关研究协议异质、资源报告不完整，缺乏对实际收益与量子优势的统一判断，因此需要系统性综述与批判性评估。

Method: 采用两条互补分类轴进行文献综述：（1）教师—学生配置；（2）量子资源在蒸馏过程中的角色。将量子启发方法、经典模拟量子计算的方法与在量子硬件上执行的方法分开讨论，并分析测量概率、期望值、经典读出、态重叠和电路级目标如何支撑不同的迁移机制；随后从基线设计、执行条件、不确定性与资源成本四个维度对结果进行批判性评估。

Result: 证据表明蒸馏能改善部分选定的量子学生模型，但收益依赖于任务与架构，若干研究报告增益可忽略甚至性能下降；在真实量子硬件上的评估仍然有限，部分工作仅测试单个量子组件而非完整学习流程；协议异质与资源报告不完整使关于实际量子优势的一般性结论无法成立。

Conclusion: 作者提出未来优先方向：受控基准测试（controlled benchmarking）、噪声感知学习、可复现评估，以及量子与经典模型之间的高效迁移。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation transfers predictions, representations, or other supervisory information between learning models and is increasingly investigated in quantum machine learning. Its applications extend beyond compression to cross-architecture transfer, circuit approximation, distributed learning, and classical deployment of models trained using quantum-derived supervision. This survey reviews these approaches using two complementary classification axes: teacher-student configuration and the role of quantum resources in the distillation process. Quantum-inspired methods are discussed separately from methods that simulate quantum computations classically or execute them on quantum hardware. We explain how measurement probabilities, expectation values, classical readouts, state overlaps, and circuit-level objectives support different transfer mechanisms. We then critically assess the presented results against baseline design, execution conditions, uncertainty, and resource costs. The evidence shows that distillation can improve selected quantum students, but benefits are task- and architecture-dependent, and several studies find negligible gains or even performance degradation. Evaluations on physical quantum hardware remain limited, with some testing individual quantum components rather than complete learning pipelines. Heterogeneous protocols and incomplete resource reporting prevent general conclusions about practical quantum advantage. We identify priorities for controlled benchmarking, noise-aware learning, reproducible evaluation, and efficient transfer between quantum and classical models.

</details>

### [100] [Confinement-induced bound state and effective potential for microwave-shielded ultracold molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07230)
*Linyi Zhang,Xiaoling Cui*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 研究纵向囚禁下微波屏蔽超冷分子的束缚态与有效势，发现囚禁诱导束缚态，并构建可准确预测结合能的二维有效势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波屏蔽超冷分子在低维几何中的物理丰富，但缺乏能准确描述纵向囚禁下束缚态的有效二维势，简化势适用范围有限。

Method: 考虑纵向囚禁形成准二维（q2D）几何，在构建有效二维势时完整纳入纵向方向的相互作用效应，并与简化二维势进行对比。

Result: 纵向囚禁促进玻色子和费米子分子的束缚态形成，产生自由空间中不存在的囚禁诱导束缚态；有效势在更宽的微波耦合与囚禁强度参数范围内准确预测结合能，甚至可预测结合能远超囚禁频率的深束缚态。

Conclusion: 所建立的有效二维势为高效探索微波屏蔽超冷分子的低维物理奠定基础。

Abstract: We investigate the bound state and effective potential of microwave-shielded ultracold molecules under longitudinal confinement, which effectively creates a quasi-two-dimensional (q2D) geometry. We find that the longitudinal confinement facilitates bound-state formation in both bosonic and fermionic molecules, leading to confinement-induced bound states that are absent in free space. To describe these bound states, we establish an effective 2D potential by incorporating the full interaction effect along the longitudinal direction. Compared with other simplified 2D potentials, the effective potential accurately predicts the binding energies of ultracold molecules over a much broader parameter regime of microwave coupling and confinement strength. Remarkably, it can even predict deep bound states with binding energies well exceeding the confinement frequency. By establishing this effective potential, our work paves the way for efficiently exploring the rich low-dimensional physics of microwave-shielded ultracold molecules.

</details>


### [101] [Initial heat-current decay versus thermal transport at a three-body resonance in a one-dimensional Fermi gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07615)
*Yixin Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 弱相互作用一维无自旋费米气体在三体共振附近的动力学弛豫与热输运：使初始热流衰减率最大的共振位置高于使积分弛豫时间最小的位置，快初始衰减可与慢后续弛豫及更大低频热响应共存，两种共振位置处的耗散响应随频率增大至少反转两次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀释一维费米气体靠近三体共振时，弛豫由完整碰撞算符决定；以往常用单一弛豫时间或初始衰减率刻画热输运，但不同时间尺度可能不一致，需厘清共振位置对初始衰减、积分弛豫时间与频率依赖耗散响应的不同影响。

Method: 采用含有限程相互作用的真空散射振幅（full vacuum amplitude）构建线性化Boltzmann碰撞算符；计算初始热流衰减率与积分弛豫时间随共振位置的变化，分析动量矩间的耦合，并利用完整碰撞算符的界（bounds）验证双重反转，考察四阶矩畸变作为动量分布探针。

Result: 最大化初始热流衰减率的共振位置高于最小化积分弛豫时间者；在更高共振能量下，动量矩耦合将热流权重转移至慢模；两共振位置处耗散响应随频率增大至少两次反转次序，碰撞算符界证实此反转；四阶矩畸变呈现相同的弛豫尺度分离。

Conclusion: 初始快衰减不意味着整体快弛豫：共振能量升高可产生"先快后慢"的弛豫层次与增强的低频热响应；热输运不能用单一弛豫时间描述，需区分初始衰减率、积分弛豫时间与频率依赖的耗散响应，四阶矩畸变为实验探针。

Abstract: We theoretically study kinetic relaxation and heat transport in a dilute one-dimensional spinless Fermi gas near a three-body resonance. The full vacuum amplitude of a finite-range interaction enters the linearized Boltzmann collision operator. The resonance position that maximizes the initial heat-current decay rate lies above the one that minimizes the integrated relaxation time. At higher resonance energies, coupling among momentum moments transfers heat-current weight to a slow mode. A faster initial decay can therefore coexist with slower subsequent relaxation and a larger low-frequency heat response. The dissipative responses at two resonance positions reverse their ordering at least twice as frequency increases. Bounds on the full collision operator confirm both reversals. A fourth-moment distortion exhibits the same separation of relaxation scales and provides a probe through the momentum distribution.

</details>


### [102] [Universal Phase Locking from Slowly Varying Inhomogeneity in Tunnel-Coupled One-Dimensional Condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07657)
*Bence Fitos,Gábor Takács*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 结合截断Wigner近似与渐近分析，证明一维隧穿耦合玻色凝聚体中非均匀密度剖面导致的相位锁定现象，其机制为密度极大值附近相对相位的普适漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正弦-戈登模型可由隧穿耦合的一维玻色凝聚体实验实现，但实验观察到系统弛豫到相位锁定态，这与均匀可积理论的预测相矛盾。作者旨在揭示这一矛盾的成因，即解释实验中相位锁定的物理起源。

Method: 采用截断Wigner近似并结合渐近分析，处理含缓慢变化密度剖面的非均匀系统动力学；同时在线性化理论框架下分析相对相位漂移的出现及其时间尺度。

Result: 相位锁定由缓慢变化的密度剖面通过普适机制产生：密度极大值附近的相对相位向零漂移。该漂移在线性化理论中已存在，其时间尺度仅由密度曲率决定，与隧穿耦合强度无关。以弛豫时间尺度重新标度时间后，不同系统尺寸与密度剖面的动力学坍缩到同一曲线上；非线性则抑制从边缘返回的激发。

Conclusion: 均匀可积正弦-戈登理论的失效源于密度非均匀性诱导的相对相位漂移，而非隧穿耦合本身；该机制具有普适性与标度不变性，可用于解释并预测隧穿耦合一维玻色凝聚体的相位锁定动力学。

Abstract: The sine-Gordon model can be experimentally realised by tunnel-coupled one-dimensional Bose condensates. However, the experiments show relaxation to a phase-locked state, which conflicts with the predictions of the homogeneous integrable theory. Here we combine the truncated Wigner approximation with an asymptotic analysis, and show that this locking is produced by a slowly varying density profile through a universal mechanism, whereby the relative phase near the density maximum drifts towards zero. We show that this drift is already present in the linearised theory and its timescale is determined by the density curvature alone, while it is independent of the tunnel coupling. Rescaling time by the relaxation time scale collapses the dynamics for different system sizes and density profiles, while non-linearity suppresses the excitation returning from the edges.

</details>


### [103] [Coherent phase slips in coupled rings: from weak to strong interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08168)
*Axel Perez-Obiol,Malcolm G. Boshier,Luigi Amico,Juan Polo*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在弱耦合的双环晶格超冷玻色子体系中，环流可在两环间相干振荡而无净粒子转移，其微观机制随相互作用强度不同分为独立的模式隧穿与二阶集体过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究持续电流在两弱耦合环间相干转移的微观机制，区分弱相互作用与强相互作用两种不同物理图像，并评估可观测性与潜在实验实现。

Method: 结合Bose–Hubbard数值模拟及其Gross–Pitaevskii与量子相位模型极限，分析环流振荡的时间尺度、粒子数涨落与纠缠结构，并提出态制备的淬火方案。

Result: 弱相互作用下，各占据动量模独立隧穿，转移时间与环间隧穿成反比，粒子数涨落广延、纠缠分布于大量Schmidt分量，且随相互作用增强被自陷抑制；强相互作用大填充时进入相位主导区，环流经多体持续电流态间的二阶集体过程转移，转移时间在弱连接极限下与隧穿强度的平方成反比，粒子数涨落保持O(1)，每个数扇区内形成两多体组态的相干叠加，保数操作下可及纠缠约达1 bit。

Conclusion: 耦合环晶格可作为研究相干量子相位滑移与操控持续电流态的平台，所提淬火方案与估计的转移时间处于现有环晶格实验可达范围。

Abstract: We study the coherent transfer of circulation between two weakly coupled lattice rings of ultracold bosons, in which a persistent current initially prepared in one ring oscillates back and forth between the rings without net transfer of particles. Combining Bose--Hubbard simulations with their Gross--Pitaevskii and quantum phase model limits, we show that similar current oscillations arise from two distinct microscopic mechanisms. At weak interactions, particles in each occupied momentum mode tunnel independently between the rings, leading to a transfer time inversely proportional to the inter-ring tunnelling, extensive particle-number fluctuations and entanglement distributed over many Schmidt components; this mode-exchange dynamics is suppressed by self-trapping as the interactions increase. At stronger interactions and large filling, in the phase-dominated regime, circulation is instead transferred through a second-order collective process between many-body persistent-current states, with a transfer time scaling as the inverse square of the inter-ring tunnelling in the weak-link limit. Number fluctuations then remain of order one instead of growing with the particle number, and within each number sector the system evolves into coherent superpositions of two many-body configurations, distinguished by which ring carries the circulation. The entanglement accessible under number-conserving operations therefore reaches up to approximately one bit. We propose a quench protocol to prepare the initial state and estimate transfer times within reach of current ring-lattice experiments, identifying coupled rings as a platform to study coherent quantum phase slips and to manipulate persistent-current states.

</details>


### [104] [Dynamical effects induced by synthetic SU(1,1) gauge fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08447)
*Chenwei Lv*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 研究 SU(1,1) 非紧规范场驱动的波包与纠缠动力学：波包出现非阿贝尔非紧 Bloch 振荡，不稳定绝热区中其放大仅由 Minkowski 空间的双曲立体角决定；该规范场作用于玻色紧束缚模型给出玻色 Kitaev 模型，其体积/超体积纠缠标度受规范场稳定性支配，并提出在旋量凝聚体中实验实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧致规范场已广泛用于调控量子动力学与拓扑物态，但开放系统中与环境交换粒子或能量的情形需要非紧规范场；非紧群（如 SU(1,1)）的动力学行为、几何图像与纠缠规律尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 构造/研究 SU(1,1) 非紧规范场下的波包演化与纠缠动力学；借助辅助一维量子问题能谱判定稳定振荡与不稳定放大的转变；在非阿贝尔非紧 Bloch 振荡框架下分析波包对 SU(1,1) 拓扑缺陷的探测，并以 Minkowski 空间双曲立体角表征放大倍数；将 SU(1,1) 规范场引入玻色紧束缚模型得到玻色 Kitaev 模型，分析纠缠标度；提出旋量凝聚体中的实验实现方案。

Result: 波包表现出非阿贝尔非紧 Bloch 振荡；稳定振荡与不稳定放大的转变由辅助一维问题能谱决定；不稳定绝热区中波包探测到 SU(1,1) 拓扑缺陷，且放大仅由 Minkowski 空间双曲立体角确定；玻色 Kitaev 模型的体积/超体积纠缠标度由规范场稳定性控制；给出旋量凝聚体中的实现方案。

Conclusion: SU(1,1) 非紧规范场提供一个几何化的统一框架，可用于纠缠控制、选择性放大与能量转移工程，并可推广至其他非紧群与更高维度。

Abstract: Compact gauge fields are powerful tools for controlling quantum dynamics and topological phases of matter. Non-compact gauge fields are particularly relevant for open systems, where particles or energy are exchanged with the environment. Here, we investigate the wavepacket and entanglement dynamics induced by an $\mathrm{SU}(1,1)$ gauge field. The wavepacket undergoes non-Abelian non-compact Bloch oscillations, in which the spectrum of an auxiliary one-dimensional quantum problem determines the transition between stable oscillations and unstable amplification. In the unstable adiabatic regime, the wavepacket probes an $\mathrm{SU}(1,1)$ topological defect, and its amplification is determined solely by a hyperbolic solid angle in Minkowski space. Threading an $\mathrm{SU}(1,1)$ gauge field through a bosonic tight-binding model yields the bosonic Kitaev model, whose volume or super-volume entanglement scaling is governed by the stability of the gauge field. We further propose an experimental realization of an $\mathrm{SU}(1,1)$ gauge field in a spinor condensate. Our results can be generalized to other non-compact groups and higher dimensions, providing a geometric framework for entanglement control, selective amplification, and engineering energy transfer.

</details>


### [105] [Flat-band systems: A platform to realize the moments of Quantum Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08479)
*Rishi Gangadhar,Binayyak Roy,Vito Scarola,Sumanta Tewari*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 提出用高斯波包在孤立平带中受力后的实空间展宽动力学来探测量子度规的动量空间变化，解析证明方差变化中有一项正比于波包平均的量子度规迹梯度，并在Lieb格子上数值验证，为平带体系中量子几何的动力学探测提供方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子度规是Bloch带的基本几何性质并支配众多物理现象，但实验上难以直接获取，尤其需要一种可操作的动力学探测量。

Method: 在孤立平带中制备高斯波包，施加弱恒定外力，解析推导波包方差随时间的领头阶变化；采用具有精确平坦中间带且Berry曲率为零的带隙Lieb格子，使量子度规贡献与色散效应分离，并进行数值模拟对照。

Result: 解析表明方差领头阶变化随时间线性，且含一项正比于波包平均的量子度规迹梯度的贡献，该几何贡献在无色散时仍非零；数值结果与解析预言一致，证实量子度规对波包展宽的贡献。

Conclusion: 波包展宽可作为平带体系中量子几何的动力学探针；所需晶格制备、可调跃迁振幅、外力和密度测量均与成熟超冷原子技术兼容，提供可行实验平台。

Abstract: The quantum metric is a fundamental geometric property of Bloch bands that governs a wide range of physical phenomena, yet experimentally accessing it remains challenging. We show that the momentum-space variation of the quantum metric can be probed through the real-space dynamics of a Gaussian wave packet prepared in an isolated flat band. Following the application of a weak constant force, we demonstrate analytically that the leading order change in the wave-packet variance is linear in time and contains a contribution proportional to the wave-packet averaged gradient of the quantum-metric trace. This geometric contribution remains finite despite the absence of band dispersion. We illustrate the effect using a gapped Lieb lattice with an exactly flat middle band and vanishing Berry curvature, allowing the quantum-metric contribution to the spreading to be isolated. The numerical results agree with the analytical prediction and confirm the quantum-metric contribution to the wave-packet spreading. The required lattice preparation, tunable hopping amplitudes, applied force, and density measurements are compatible with established ultracold-atom techniques, providing a possible platform for observing the predicted response. Our results establish wave-packet spreading as a dynamical probe of quantum geometry in flat-band systems.

</details>

### [106] [Nonequilibrium Spin-Glass Order in a Long-Range Quantum Ising Glass and Its Sensitivity to Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07115)
*Giuseppe D'Auria,Alessandro Silva*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究了长程量子Ising自旋玻璃在横场淬火（quench）后的自旋玻璃序：对角系综Edwards-Anderson序参量在无淬火端与零场端取值相同，但中间区域"冷壳层"过冲、"热壳层"不足，源于关联空间中占据本征态的刚度与成对模式展宽之间的竞争；弱纵向退相位下零场端点被精确钉扎而有限淬火响应消退，可作为量子自旋模拟器的自参考噪声探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淬火动力学后自旋玻璃序如何演化、以及序参量能否作为可标定的测量量，是量子自旋模拟器中的核心问题；本文旨在厘清横场淬火下Edwards-Anderson序参量的行为及其对退相位的稳健性。

Method: 在长程量子Ising玻璃模型中施加横场淬火，采用对角系综（diagonal ensemble）计算Edwards-Anderson序参量，并按本征态壳层（"冷"/"热"）分解，分析关联空间中本征态刚性与成对模式展宽的竞争；进一步引入弱纵向退相位考察端点钉扎效应。

Result: 对角系综Edwards-Anderson序参量在无淬火端与零场端重合，但中间淬火幅度处出现非单调行为：冷壳层过冲、热壳层不足。该行为由占据本征态的刚度与其成对模式展宽之间的竞争主导。弱纵向退相位下，零场端点数值被精确钉扎，而有限淬火的特征响应逐渐消失。

Conclusion: 淬火后的自旋玻璃序不能由单一参量单调刻画，需区分本征态壳层贡献；零场端点的退相位稳健性使该响应可作为量子自旋模拟器的自参考噪声探针。

Abstract: We study spin-glass order after a transverse-field quench in a long-range quantum Ising glass. The diagonal-ensemble Edwards-Anderson order coincides at the no-quench and zero-field endpoints, yet in between cold shells overshoot it and hot shells undershoot it. This stems from a competition in correlation space between the rigidity of populated eigenstates and the spread of their pair patterns. Under weak longitudinal dephasing the zero-field endpoint is exactly pinned while the characteristic finite-quench response fades, giving a self-referenced noise probe for quantum spin simulators.

</details>


### [107] [On the critical value of void percolation in high dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07196)
*Patrick Charbonneau,Marcus Michelen*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文证明高维随机Lorentz气体的空穴渗流阈值满足 Φ_p(d)=Θ(d)，即对充分大的 d 有 d/2 ≤ Φ_p(d) ≤ 5d，从而确定了其维度标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空穴渗流阈值的维度标度长期未定：先前的界（含猜想）从 Ω(1) 到 O(d ln d) 不等，连标度本身都不确定；这直接关系到高维中平均场动力学转变之上"激活窗口"是否发散的问题。

Method: 上界采用对分层"树嵌入"（tree embeddings）逃逸路标家族进行计数；下界在显式弹道逃逸路线集合上做加权的第二矩估计。

Result: 证明 Φ_p(d)=Θ(d)，具体给出对所有充分大 d 的 d/2 ≤ Φ_p(d) ≤ 5d，与数值估计 Φ_p/d→3.40(5) 一致，并排除了 d ln d 增长。

Conclusion: 线性标度排除随机一阶转变普适类中其他系统所提示的 d ln d 增长；由于平均场动力学转变也是线性的（Φ_d≃2.40d），激活窗口 [Φ_d,Φ_p] 在高维下成为有界修正而非发散。

Abstract: In the random Lorentz gas, a point tracer moves through spherical obstacles of (reduced) density $Φ$ centered on a Poisson point process in $d$-dimensional space, $\mathbb{R}^d$, and becomes localized once the vacant space stops percolating, at $Φ_{\rm p}(d)$. The dimensional scaling of this void percolation threshold has remained elusive. Prior bounds, conjectural ones included, ranged from $Ω(1)$ to $O(d\ln d)$, leaving even the scaling undetermined. We here prove that $Φ_{\rm p}(d)=Θ(d)$, and in particular that $\tfrac12\,d \le Φ_{\rm p}(d) \le 5\,d$ for all sufficiently large $d$. The upper bound counts hierarchical families of escape waypoints called tree embeddings; the lower bound is via a weighted second-moment estimate on an explicit ensemble of ballistic escape routes. A linear scaling of $Φ_{\rm p}(d)$ excludes the $d\ln d$ growth suggested by analogy with other systems in the random first-order transition universality class and is consistent with the numerical estimate $Φ_{\rm p}/d \to 3.40(5)$. Because the mean-field dynamical transition is likewise linear, $Φ_{\rm d}\simeq 2.40\,d$, our result fixes the activated window $[Φ_{\rm d},Φ_{\rm p}]$ as a bounded---rather than diverging---correction to mean-field dynamical arrest in sufficiently high $d$.

</details>


### [108] [Thermodynamics of Forgetting in a Bounded Hopfield Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08258)
*Andrea Lepre*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文给出Hopfield-Parisi模型（带突触强度截断的遗忘型联想记忆网络）的热力学描述：证明其等价于"按年龄加权的Hopfield模型+SKK自旋玻璃"的叠加，用Guerra插值在复制对称层面重新推导并首次在有限温度下求解，得到相图与记忆视野，并用Monte Carlo模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Hopfield网络存储模式数超过临界值后会陷入完全混乱、无法检索任何记忆，缺乏"边学边忘"的能力。Parisi (1986) 指出限制突触强度（coupling clipping）可治愈该病理：耦合截断会逐渐抹去最旧的记忆、为新记忆腾出空间。然而该Hopfield-Parisi模型的理论方程虽在八十年代经启发式复制方法给出，却仅被求解于零温情形，缺乏有限温度的热力学与相图认识。

Method: 从学习规则出发，将网络重写为"按年龄加权的Hopfield模式"与Sherrington-Kirkpatrick自旋玻璃的叠加，二者共同受突触界约束；采用Guerra插值方法在复制对称（RS）层面重新推导序参量方程，并在有限温度下数值求解，得到相图与记忆视野（模式仍可被检索的最大年龄）；同时用原始学习规则训练的网络的Monte Carlo模拟进行检验。

Result: 获得模型的完整相图及记忆视野随温度的依赖；结果显示遗忘仅作用于信号项，而各年龄模式的噪声水平相同；记忆视野在某个中间取值的突触界处达到最大，且该最优界对温度基本不敏感。原始学习规则训练的网络的Monte Carlo模拟结果与理论预言一致。

Conclusion: Hopfield-Parisi网络可被完整地以热力学方式刻画：其本质是年龄加权Hopfield信号与SK噪声的竞争，遗忘是纯信号衰减效应；突触界越大记忆越长但噪声越强，存在与温度几乎无关的最优界。该工作把八十年代表征的非线性网络理论从零温推广到有限温度，为持续学习型联想记忆的设计提供了定量依据。

Abstract: A memory that keeps learning must also forget. The Hopfield network does not: beyond a critical number of stored patterns it falls into a state of total confusion and retrieves none of them. Parisi showed in 1986 that bounding the synaptic strength cures this pathology, since the clipping of the couplings gradually erases the oldest memories and leaves room for the new ones. In this paper we provide a thermodynamic description of the resulting network, known as the Hopfield-Parisi model. Starting from the learning rule, we show that the network is equivalent to a Hopfield model whose patterns are weighted according to their age, superimposed on a Sherrington-Kirkpatrick spin glass, both governed by the synaptic bound: a larger bound makes the memory longer, but also noisier. The theory of nonlinear networks developed in the eighties already provides, through the heuristic replica method, the equations that govern this model at any temperature, but to the best of our knowledge they have been solved only at zero temperature. We rederive them at the replica symmetric level through Guerra's interpolation and solve them at finite temperature, obtaining the phase diagram of the model together with its memory horizon, namely the largest age at which a pattern can still be retrieved. The solution shows that forgetting acts on the signal alone, the noise being the same for patterns of every age, and that the horizon is maximal at an intermediate bound which is essentially insensitive to temperature. Monte Carlo simulations of networks trained with the original learning rule support these predictions.

</details>

### [109] [Stability and Relaxation of Electron-Phonon Systems at Strong Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07014)
*Noga Bashan,Erez Berg*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文用准经典Kadanoff-Baym方程研究强耦合下Migdal-Eliashberg理论(MET)的动力学性质，发现马尔可夫量子玻尔兹曼近似下的动力学不稳定性在计入非马尔可夫效应后消失，并揭示了普朗克线性弛豫与强振荡衰减特征，表明声子频率并非Tc的根本上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前在马尔可夫量子玻尔兹曼近似下，强电声耦合的MET体系被判定存在动力学不稳定性，这可能预先排除超导；作者希望厘清该不稳定性是物理本质还是近似假象，并考察强耦合下的弛豫动力学。

Method: 采用准经典Kadanoff-Baym方程（而非马尔可夫量子玻尔兹曼近似）研究强耦合电子-声子相互作用下的MET动力学，计入非马尔可夫记忆效应，并分析长时间弛豫行为。

Result: 非马尔可夫效应使原不稳定消失；在德拜频率与耦合强度之间的中间温区，弛豫速率为线性温度依赖且呈普朗克斜率，主导弛豫模式获得大的虚部，导致强振荡衰减。

Conclusion: 任何先于超导出现的不稳定性必须源于MET之外（如声子软化或有限电子带宽下的极化子效应）；特征声子频率并非Tc的基本上限，多轨道电声系统存在提高Tc的途径。

Abstract: We study the kinetic properties of Migdal-Eliashberg theory (MET) for electron-phonon interactions at strong coupling. Previously, the system was found to be dynamically unstable at large couplings within a Markovian quantum Boltzmann approximation. Using the quasiclassical Kadanoff-Baym equation, we show that this instability is absent when non-Markovian effects are taken into account. We also analyze the long-time relaxation dynamics at strong-couplin, and find a clear imprint of the non-Markovian nature of the dynamics: in an intermediate temperature range between the Debye frequency and the coupling strength, the relaxation rate becomes linear in temperature with Planckian slope, while the leading relaxation mode acquires a large imaginary component, producing strongly oscillatory decay. Our results imply that any instability which preempts superconductivity must arise from effects beyond MET, such as phonon softening or polaronic effects at finite electronic bandwidth. This shows that the characteristic phonon frequency is not a fundamental bound on $T_c$, and leaves open a route to higher $T_c$ in multi-orbital electron-phonon systems.

</details>


### [110] [Agnostic and efficient detection of $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry-protected topological phases in projected entangled-pair states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07030)
*Alexandre Chaduteau,Hao Chen,Juraj Hasik,Titus Neupert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种在热力学极限下、与虚拟对称表示及键维无关的高效方法，用于检测相互作用二维Z₂ SPT相，并从PEPS张量中显式提取SPT标签的三上闭链（cocycle）数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPT相分类已较完善，但真正的实际问题是"检测"：给定相互作用哈密顿量基态、对称群G及其表示，能否高效判断该态是否处于相对G的SPT相。一维已有实用判据，而二维方法此前受限于有限尺寸体系，或需预先知道张量网络的虚拟对称性显式形式。

Method: 采用无限投影纠缠对态（iPEPS）表示基态，构造不依赖虚拟空间对称表示、与键维无关的检测流程；直接在热力学极限下工作，无需有限尺寸标度分析；从PEPS张量中显式提取SPT标签的cocycle数据。

Result: 数值演示了该检测流程，能在偏离不动点波函数处描绘出相边界，并成功从PEPS张量中显式提取出Z₂ SPT的cocycle标签数据。

Conclusion: 所提方法为二维相互作用SPT相提供了实用、高效且"不可知"（agnostic）的检测手段，弥补了二维情形下现有方法的不足，可直接在无限体系中判别SPT相并刻画相变。

Abstract: Symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases are the simplest example of quantum phases that cannot be detected by local order parameters. Their classification is by now well-established from multiple perspectives, a prominent one being via tensor network states. The practical question, however, is not mere classification, but detection: given the ground state of an interacting Hamiltonian and a symmetry group $G$ along with its representation, is there an efficient way to tell if the state is in a SPT phase with respect to $G$? While there exist such practical tests in one spatial dimension (1D), methods in 2D have so far been restricted to finite-size systems or require an explicit form of virtual symmetries of the tensor networks representing the ground state. Here, we develop an agnostic and efficient method to detect interacting $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 2D SPT phases, using an infinite projected entangled-pair (PEPS) representation of the ground state. The method is agnostic to the symmetry representation in the virtual space of the PEPS tensor and applicable independent of the bond dimension of the tensors. Moreover, it is efficient as it does not require finite-size scalings: it works directly in the thermodynamic limit. We numerically demonstrate the procedure by mapping out phase boundaries away from fixed-point wavefunctions. We extract the SPT label cocycle data explicitly from the PEPS tensor.

</details>


### [111] [Exact magnetic ground states and competing interlayer exchanges in layered kagome magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07131)
*Vladimir Antropov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用含六个层间交换作用的Heisenberg模型研究层状kagome磁体的磁基态，证明在交换函数相同的各向同性双亚晶格模型中，任意交换范围内均存在共面单波矢基态；将Rosenfeld–Mushnikov三交换模型扩展后发现长程交换可使磁序在螺旋—铁磁—螺旋之间转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状kagome磁体的磁基态通常由少数层间交换常数近似描述（如三交换模型），但长程交换作用如何影响磁序类型（而非仅改变螺旋周期）尚不清楚，且可观测物理量能否唯一确定全部交换常数缺乏系统分析。

Method: 构建含六个层间交换作用的Heisenberg模型；针对各向同性双亚晶格情形分析对角Fourier交换函数；扩展Rosenfeld–Mushnikov三交换模型以纳入更长程交换；计算基态相图、谐波稳定性、磁场响应及各交换常数的敏感性。

Result: 证明对任意交换范围存在共面单波矢基态；发现长程交换可驱动磁序从螺旋变为铁磁再回到螺旋；给出基态相图与稳定性分析；并表明传播矢量、层内夹角、饱和场和面内磁化率不足以唯一确定六个交换常数。

Conclusion: 长程层间交换定性改变基态磁序类型，需完整的多交换常数框架；所提方法为从第一性原理交换作用预测非共线磁序提供了基础。

Abstract: Magnetic ground states in layered kagome magnets are investigated using a Heisenberg model with six interlayer exchange interactions. For isotropic two-sublattice models with identical diagonal Fourier exchange functions, a coplanar single-wave-vector ground state is shown to exist for arbitrary exchange ranges. Extending the three-exchange model of Rosenfeld and Mushnikov reveals that longer-range interactions can change the preferred magnetic order from spiral to ferromagnetic and back to spiral, rather than merely modifying the spiral period. The analysis includes ground-state phase diagrams, harmonic stability, magnetic-field response, and sensitivity to individual exchange interactions. The propagation vector, internal layer angle, saturation field, and planar susceptibility are also found to be insufficient to determine all six exchange constants uniquely. These results provide a basis for predicting noncollinear magnetic order from first-principles exchange interactions.

</details>


### [112] [Observable-Based Learning of Effective Entanglement Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07353)
*Siyi Yang,Yuzhi Liu,Yi-Ming Ding,Zheng Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出"可观测量匹配"框架，利用量子蒙特卡洛的响应估计学习相互作用体系的纠缠哈密顿量，可校准并超越格点Bisognano-Wichmann拟设，在(2+1)D O(3) Wilson-Fisher临界点获得von Neumann熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用量子多体体系的纠缠哈密顿量（EH）除少数可解析情形外难以确定；既有LBW构造依赖预设的空间耦合剖面，缺乏对空间依赖形式的检验与推广。

Method: 构建可观测量匹配框架：以QMC给出的响应估计为目标，先以等时关联校准LBW构造的整体前因子，再在不假设具体函数形式下学习空间分辨的耦合；仅用局域关联训练。

Result: 学习到的EH在一维复现benchmark的von Neumann熵与二阶Rényi熵，在二维复现二阶Rényi熵；在(2+1)D O(3) Wilson-Fisher临界点的强键切割（特殊表面临界性）处，自由形式学习在预测二阶Rényi熵上优于校准的LBW拟设，并揭示非均匀的耦合重整化；该EH还可给出von Neumann熵，显示负的次领头修正。

Conclusion: 基于可观测量的学习为在更高维相互作用体系中构造超越预设空间剖面的有效纠缠哈密顿量提供了可行途径，并拓展了常规数值方法难以直接计算的von Neumann熵结果。

Abstract: Determining the entanglement Hamiltonian (EH) of an interacting quantum many-body system remains a fundamental challenge beyond special analytically tractable cases. Here we develop an observable-matching framework for learning effective EHs, which we demonstrate using response estimates from quantum Monte Carlo. As applications of this framework, we first calibrate the overall prefactor of the lattice Bisognano--Wichmann (LBW) construction using equal-time correlations and then learn spatially resolved couplings beyond its prescribed profile, without assuming a specific functional form for their spatial dependence. Trained solely on local correlations, the learned EHs reproduce benchmarked von Neumann and second Rényi entropies in $1$D and second Rényi entropies in $2$D. For a strong-bond cut associated with special surface criticality at the $(2+1)$D $O(3)$ Wilson--Fisher critical point, free-form learning outperforms the calibrated LBW ansatz in predicting the second Rényi entropy and reveals spatially nonuniform coupling renormalizations. Importantly, the learned EH also provides access to the von Neumann entropy in this setting, revealing a negative subleading correction and extending previous Rényi entropy findings to the von Neumann case. Directly evaluating the von Neumann entropy in such $(2+1)$D interacting quantum many-body systems remains challenging for conventional numerical methods. These results establish observable-based learning as a route to constructing effective entanglement Hamiltonians beyond prescribed spatial profiles in higher-dimensional interacting systems.

</details>


### [113] [Extensions of the Haldane gap proof: higher dimensions, near degeneracy, fermions and tensor-network states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07400)
*Tarun Grover*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文推广OpenAI关于一维自旋1海森堡链谱隙证明中的"纯度自举"论证，将其扩展至任意空间维度、费米子系统与张量网络态，从有限系统信息导出热力学极限下的低能谱结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenAI近期证明周期边界一维自旋1海森堡链在热力学极限下存在谱隙，其核心是从有限系统谱推断极限谱隙的纯度不等式论证。作者希望考察这一基本思想的可推广性，检验其能否超越一维玻色自旋链的原始设定。

Method: 以纯度自举不等式为基础，利用对称性生成元与对称性twist之间的时空对偶，从有限系统信息得到近简并低能态劈裂的上界及其上方非零能隙；随后分别推广到任意空间维度、费米子系统以及张量网络态。

Result: 对2+1维含场环面码，有限计算确立存在四个低能态，劈裂上界为e^{-cL}；对2+1维横场Ising模型，确立两个低能态，劈裂上界为e^{-cL^2}，二者上方能隙在热力学极限下均非零。费米子情形下，通过有限计算在2+1维费米子模型中确立对称质量生成相。对张量网络态，证明Jian–Zaletel所引入对称正方晶格自旋1态中所有局域算符连通关联函数的指数衰减。

Conclusion: 纯度自举是超越原始一维海森堡链设定的普适工具，可系统推广至任意维度、费米子体系与张量网络态，并给出可严格验证的极限谱结构与关联衰减结论。

Abstract: A recent remarkable paper by OpenAI showed that the spin-one Heisenberg chain with periodic boundary conditions has a spectral gap in the thermodynamic limit. The proof elegantly deduces this gap from the Hamiltonian's spectrum on a finite system. This basic idea is an inequality involving the purity of the original Gibbs state and a dual Gibbs state that is defined via space-time rotation. Here we consider a few generalizations of this purity bootstrap argument. We first extend it to arbitrary spatial dimensions. Our main result uses the space-time duality between symmetry generators and symmetry twists to obtain upper bounds on the splitting of nearly degenerate low-energy states, together with a nonzero gap above them, from finite-system information. As applications, we show that finite-system calculations for the 2+1-D toric code in a field and the 2+1-D transverse-field Ising model establish parameter regimes with four and two low-lying states whose splittings are bounded from above by $e^{-cL}$ and $e^{-cL^2}$ respectively for arbitrarily large system size $L$, while the gap above these states remains nonzero in the thermodynamic limit. Next, we extend it to fermionic systems and use a finite calculation to establish a symmetric mass generation phase in a $2+1$-D fermionic model. Finally, we apply the bootstrap to tensor-network states and establish exponential decay of connected correlations of all local operators in the symmetric square-lattice spin-one state introduced by Jian and Zaletel.

</details>


### [114] [Interfacial polarity control of anomalous Hall effect in LaCoO3-SrIrO3 heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07501)
*Yeonjae Lee,Chanyoung Shin,Youngdo Kim,Jinyoung Kim,Jaeung Lee,Miyoung Kim,Sangjae Lee,Changyoung Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过独立调控层堆叠顺序与原子终止面来控制SrIrO3-LaCoO3界面极性诱导电荷的符号，原位ARPES证实带电符号反转，磁输运表明AHE仅在电子掺杂下出现、空穴掺杂下被抑制，机制为极性调控能带填充所主导的Berry曲率驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 界面极性可驱动跨界面电荷重分布，但其在调控拓扑输运（如反常霍尔效应AHE）中的作用尚未被充分探索，本文希望将界面极性由被动结构结果转变为主动设计变量。

Method: 在SrIrO3-LaCoO3界面中，通过独立设计层堆叠顺序与原子终止面调控极性诱导电荷的符号；用原位角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）对比不同界面构型下的能带位移；对四种异质结构构成的系统矩阵进行磁输运测量。

Result: ARPES直接确认SrIrO3中被诱导电荷的符号反转；磁输运显示AHE可被干净地"开/关"调控——仅在电子掺杂条件下出现，空穴掺杂时被抑制。

Conclusion: 该AHE响应源于由极性控制的SrIrO3能带填充所决定的本征Berry曲率机制；界面极性可作为在氧化物异质结构中工程化拓扑量子态的主动设计变量。

Abstract: Interfacial polarity in oxide heterostructures can drive robust charge redistribution across the interface, yet its role in tailoring topological transport phenomena like the anomalous Hall effect (AHE) remains largely unexplored. Here, we control the sign of polarity-induced charge in SrIrO3-LaCoO3 interfaces by independently engineering the layer stacking sequence and the atomic termination. In-situ angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy directly confirms the sign reversal of the induced charge in SrIrO3 through the contrasting electronic band shifts between different interfacial configurations. Magneto-transport measurements across a systematic matrix of four distinct heterostructures reveal that the AHE can be cleanly toggled on and off: it emerges exclusively under electron-doped conditions, while remaining suppressed for hole-doped SrIrO3. Our results establish that this response originates from an intrinsic, Berry-curvature-driven mechanism governed by polarity-controlled band filling of SrIrO3. These findings shift interfacial polarity from a passive structural consequence to an active design variable for engineering topological quantum states in oxide heterostructures.

</details>


### [115] [Negligible Kitaev interaction in the van der Waals antiferromagnet NiBr$_2$ revealed by neutron spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07597)
*Hao Xu,Shufan Cheng,Yanyan Shangguan,Song Bao,Junbo Liao,Bo Zhang,Zihang Song,Shuai Dong,Maofeng Wu,Shinichi Itoh,Daichi Ueta,Jinsheng Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用飞行时间中子谱学测量NiBr₂的磁激发，发现其磁振子色散可由含至第三近邻Heisenberg交换、层间耦合与单离子各向异性的最小双线性模型定量描述，无需引入Kitaev相互作用，从而将NiBr₂确立为Heisenberg主导的范德华反铁磁体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NiX₂（X=卤素）家族中键依赖相互作用的来源与强度尚待厘清：NiI₂被认为存在显著Kitaev项，而更轻配体是否削弱配体介导的自旋轨道耦合、从而抑制Kitaev项，需要直接的磁激发实验证据。

Method: 对范德华反铁磁体NiBr₂进行宽能-动量范围的飞行时间非弹性中子散射测量，获得磁振子色散；并以仅包含最近邻至第三近邻Heisenberg交换、层间耦合和单离子各向异性项的最小双线性自旋哈密顿量，采用线性自旋波理论进行定量拟合。

Result: 观测到定义良好的磁振子色散，线性自旋波理论在最小双线性模型下即可定量重现实验数据，拟合中无需引入Kitaev相互作用项。

Conclusion: NiBr₂是Heisenberg主导的范德华反铁磁体；溴较碘更轻导致配体介导自旋轨道耦合减弱、Kitaev项被抑制，支持配体自旋轨道耦合决定NiX₂家族中键依赖相互作用强度的观点。

Abstract: Using time-of-flight neutron spectroscopy, we have mapped out the magnetic excitations in the van der Waals antiferromagnet NiBr$_2$ over a broad range of energies and momenta. The observed well-defined magnon dispersions are quantitatively reproduced by linear spin-wave theory based on a minimal bilinear Hamiltonian that includes Heisenberg exchange interactions up to third-nearest neighbors, interlayer couplings, and a single-ion anisotropy term, with no need to invoke a Kitaev interaction. This finding is consistent with the fact that bromine is lighter than iodine, leading to weaker ligand-mediated spin-orbit coupling in NiBr2 than in NiI2, and consequently a reduced Kitaev interaction. Our results establish NiBr$_2$ as a Heisenberg-dominated van der Waals antiferromagnet and support the view that the ligand spin-orbit coupling governs the strength of the bond-dependent interactions within the NiX$_2$ (X = halogen) family.

</details>


### [116] [Ground-state properties of uniaxial magnetic model for antiferromagnetic 1/1 periodic approximants to icosahedral quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07660)
*Shinji Watanabe*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文对二十面体准晶1/1近似相中单轴磁性模型（最近邻J1、次近邻J2反铁磁）的基态性质进行理论分析，通过数值精确计算给出基态相图，识别出多种非共线磁结构及其磁空间群与磁传播矢量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土基1/1近似相中的磁性是理解准晶磁性的关键，但各向异性的晶体电场效应与竞争交换相互作用如何决定基态磁结构尚不清楚，且不同基态常具有简并度，需要考虑磁畴对实验观测的影响。

Method: 构建1/1近似相上的单轴反铁磁J1-J2模型，引入镜面内反映晶体电场效应的磁各向异性参数；通过数值精确（穷举/精确对角化类）计算，确定以J2/J1与各向异性为坐标轴的基态相图，并用磁空间群与磁传播矢量表征各基态，分析简并度及拓扑性质。

Result: 在相图中发现多种非共线磁结构：磁空间群为I_Pm'3'与R_R-3、磁传播矢量(111)的反铁磁序；Im'm'm'与R-3'、传播矢量(000)的反铁磁序；以及铁磁序Im'm'2、I2'2'2、P2'/m'、P-1。并揭示了各基态的磁学与拓扑性质。

Conclusion: J1-J2竞争与合作各向异性共同产生丰富的非共线、多简并基态；所得磁空间群与拓扑特征可用于解析稀土基1/1近似相的实验观测，尤其便于考虑磁畴效应。

Abstract: The ground-state properties of the uniaxial magnetic model in the 1/1 periodic approximant to the icosahedral quasicrystal with antiferromagnetic interactions for the nearest-neighbor bond $J_1$ and the next-nearest-neighbor bond $J_2$ are analyzed theoretically. By performing numerically-exact calculation, we have determined the ground-state phase diagram for the interaction strength $J_2/J_1$ and the magnetic anisotropy defined within the mirror plane reflecting the effect of the crystalline electric field. We have discovered non-collinear magnetic structures, whose magnetic space groups are identified as the antiferromagnetic orders $I_{\rm P}m'\bar{3}'$ and $R_{\rm R}\bar{3}$ with the magnetic propagation vector $(111)$ as well as the antiferromagnetic orders $Im'm'm'$ and $R\bar{3}'$ with the magnetic propagation vector $(000)$ and the ferrimagnetic orders $Im'm'2$, $I2'2'2$, $P2'/m'$, and $P\bar{1}$. By analyzing each ground state with degeneracy, we have revealed the magnetic as well as topological properties, which are useful to analyze experimental observations of rare-earth-based 1/1 approximants considering the effect of magnetic domains.

</details>


### [117] [Photoinduced switching of orbital-selective Mott states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07681)
*M. Lisiecki,P. Werner,J. Herbrych*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在二轨道Hubbard-Kanamori模型中，利用矩阵乘积态方法，通过定制脉冲激发诱导轨道间电荷转移，可将初始处于轨道选择性Mott相的轨道占据近乎反转，实现光驱动的轨道选择性反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轨道体系中的轨道选择性（orbital selectivity）通常被视为一种稳态平衡性质，如何主动、可控地操控甚至反转这种选择性、并进入长寿命的非平衡态，尚缺乏微观机制层面的理解。

Method: 采用矩阵乘积态（MPS）数值方法研究两轨道Hubbard-Kanamori模型；从轨道选择性Mott相出发，施加定制光脉冲激发，追踪轨道间电荷转移、单粒子谱快照，并分析单光子和双光子激发通道，以及低频共振附近的啁啾（chirping）效应。

Result: 定制脉冲激发驱动的轨道间电荷转移使轨道占据近乎反转；单粒子谱快照显示两轨道的局域化与巡游性（localized/itinerant character）发生互换；低频共振附近的啁啾可增强电荷转移；并区分了相关的一光子与两光子激发通道。

Conclusion: 研究建立了反转轨道选择性、获得多轨道体系中长寿命非平衡态的微观途径，表明光脉冲可作为调控轨道选择性的有效手段。

Abstract: We investigate light-driven inversion of orbital selectivity in a two-orbital Hubbard-Kanamori model initially in an orbital-selective Mott phase. Using the matrix-product-state we show that tailored pulse excitations drive interorbital charge transfer that nearly reverses the orbital occupations. Single-particle spectral snapshots exhibit changes consistent with an interchange of the localized and itinerant characters of the orbitals. We analyze the relevant one- and two-photon excitation channels and show that chirping around the lower-frequency resonance enhances the transfer. These results establish a microscopic route to reversing orbital selectivity and accessing long-lived nonequilibrium states in multiorbital systems.

</details>


### [118] [Contrasting fate of holes introduced by Fe or In in kagome metal CoSn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07848)
*Véronique Brouet,Laure Thomarat,Patrick Le Fèvre,Ethan Saiman,Jean-Pascal Rueff,Oleg Rubel,Victor Balédent,François Bertran*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过In替代Sn与Fe替代Co两种空穴掺杂路径调控CoSn笼目平带位置，ARPES与XAS显示In替代导致能带刚性上移、Fe替代却反常下移，DFT将其归因于Fe局域磁矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoSn的笼目平带位于费米能级以下约0.2 eV，若能将平带移至费米能级附近，可为研究平带对电子性质的影响提供难得机会，因此需要考察引入空穴的有效途径。

Method: 采用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）与Co K边X射线吸收谱，实验比较Co/Fe与Sn/In两种替代方式引入空穴的效果；并结合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算进行对照解释。

Result: In替代Sn使能带近乎刚性地上移靠近费米能级，符合空穴掺杂预期；Fe替代Co却引起意外的能带下移。DFT重现了这两种相反趋势，并将Fe的反常行为归因于其局域磁矩：磁矩将空穴束缚在Fe杂质上，并对邻近Co原子施加吸引性静电势。

Conclusion: 两种替代均使弱色散态靠近费米能级：In替代时趋近Co笼目晶格的平带，Fe替代时则形成围绕磁性Fe杂质的部分局域态。局域磁矩可显著改变掺杂能带的移动方向，是调控平带位置时不可忽略的因素。

Abstract: CoSn is a kagome metal with well-defined flat bands 0.2 eV below the Fermi level EF. Moving them across EF would offer a rare opportunity to investigate their impact on electronic properties. Using angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) and X-ray absorption at the Co K-edge, we examine two routes to introduce holes in this compound, through Co/Fe or Sn/In substitutions. Substituting In for Sn produces an almost rigid upward shift of the bands toward EF, as expected for hole doping, whereas substituting Fe for Co leads to an unexpected downward shift. Density functional theory calculations reproduce these opposite trends and trace the unusual behavior of Fe to its local magnetic moment. The moment keeps the hole on the Fe impurity and imposes an attractive electrostatic potential on neighboring Co atoms. Overall, both substitutions bring weakly dispersing states close to EF, the flat bands of the Co kagome lattice upon In substitution and partly localized states around magnetic Fe impurities upon Fe substitution.

</details>


### [119] [Non-Perturbative Resolution of Luttinger's Theorem Violations in Strongly Correlated Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07933)
*Nazzareno Africani,Herbert Eßl,Alessandro Toschi,Michele Fabrizio*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文对强关联体系中Luttinger定理的"失配"（Luttinger积分）给出非微扰解，证明它并非反常，而是一个与Luttinger-Ward泛函Φ[G]直接相关的真正拓扑不变量，且完全来源于Matsubara轨迹在零频处的跳变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联体系中Luttinger定理的违反是一个长期悖论：粒子数本应与格林函数对数单值性（Luttinger计数）一致，并经由Streda公式联系霍尔电导；但已有报道称该关系在强关联下失效，同时指责骨架图表示（skeleton diagram）的自洽性。

Method: 采用非微扰分析：论证Luttinger定理的违反并不需要骨架图表示失效；区分粒子数与Luttinger计数之间的差值（Luttinger积分），并研究其拓扑起源，将其与Luttinger-Ward泛函Φ[G]而非单独的格林函数G相关联。

Result: 证明Luttinger积分是真正意义上的拓扑不变量，直接源于Φ[G]；且该不变量与ln G(iε)的单值性同源，均只来自Matsubara轨迹在零频处的不连续跳变。

Conclusion: Luttinger定理的"违反"可在不牺牲骨架图展开的前提下自洽解释；表观违约由拓扑性Luttinger积分承载，从而化解了多体理论中长期存在的争议。

Abstract: Many-body physics faces an apparent paradox concerning violations of Luttinger's theorem in strongly correlated systems. The theorem relates the electron number to the Luttinger count -- the monodromy of the logarithm of the imaginary-frequency Green's function $G(iε)$ -- and, at finite magnetic field, connects the Hall conductivity to the magnetic-field derivative of the Luttinger count via the Streda formula. Here, we provide a non-perturbative solution to this paradox. We demonstrate that the reported violations of Luttinger's theorem do not require a breakdown of the skeleton diagram representation of physical quantities, thereby resolving a long-standing point of contention in many-body theory. Furthermore, we show that the Luttinger integral, which accounts for the mismatch between the particle number and the Luttinger count, is itself a genuine topological invariant directly tied to the Luttinger-Ward functional $Φ[G]$ rather than to the Green's function alone. We demonstrate that this invariant, like the monodromy of $\ln G(iε)$ itself, stems exclusively from the zero-frequency discontinuity of the Matsubara trajectory.

</details>


### [120] [Key role of orbital exchange in CoO magnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08065)
*Leonid V. Pourovskii,Yuriy Mokrousov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文用DFT+DMFT的force-theorem方法构建了CoO的完整低能有效哈密顿量，包含四极轨道交换项，并揭示其在磁矩倾斜、激发谱分裂及INS柱状结构中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoO反铁磁相中Co离子具有显著轨道磁矩，有望用于轨道电子学，但涉及轨道自由度的格点间交换相互作用缺乏定量刻画，其在CoO磁性中的作用也不清楚。

Method: 基于DFT+动力学平均场理论（DMFT），采用从头算force-theorem方法推导包含轨道交换项的低能有效哈密顿量；由该哈密顿量求解共线反铁磁序；并直接对非弹性中子散射（INS）强度进行建模。

Result: 发现相当大的轨道相互作用，最近邻主导项为四极轨道交换；所得共线反铁磁序磁矩偏离赝立方<001>方向，轨道贡献约占总磁矩三分之一，与实验估计一致；磁矩倾斜源于轨道与自旋-轨道交换的共同作用；最低激发带出现显著分裂；INS柱状结构被证明是轨道交换的清晰指纹。

Conclusion: 轨道交换是理解CoO磁性与激发谱的关键要素，为复杂集体激发、其输运性质及轨道电子学应用提供启示。

Abstract: Co ions in the antiferromagnetic phase of CoO are known to possess a substantial orbital moment, making this material promising for orbitronic applications. However, intersite exchange interactions involving these orbital degrees of freedom remain poorly quantified, and their role in CoO magnetism is unclear. Here, we derive a full low-energy effective Hamiltonian for CoO, including orbital exchange terms, using an ab initio force-theorem approach based on DFT+dynamical mean-field theory. We find sizable orbital interactions, with quadrupolar orbital exchange emerging as the dominant nearest-neighbor term. The collinear antiferromagnetic order obtained from the effective Hamiltonian features magnetic moments tilted away from the pseudocubic <001> direction and an orbital contribution amounting to about one-third of the total moment, in agreement with experimental estimates. We show that the moment tilting originates from the combined effect of orbital and spin-orbital exchange interactions. The same interactions also produce a prominent splitting of the lowest excitation band. By directly modeling the inelastic neutron scattering (INS) intensity, we further show that the characteristic column-like structures previously observed in CoO INS provide a clear fingerprint of orbital exchange. We also discuss possible impact of our findings for further advances in the field of complex collective excitations, their transport properties and applications in the realm of orbitronics.

</details>


### [121] [Spin supersolid in triangular lattice easy-axis spin-1/2 antiferromagnet: a fermionic parton perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08270)
*Meng Zeng,Roderich Moessner,Shu Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 用基于π-通量狄拉克自旋液体的费米子部分子平均场理论结合RPA，研究三角晶格自旋-1/2易轴XXZ反铁磁体的超固态序，得到Y与Y'态、能隙型赝戈德斯通模及M点软模，并与数值和实验结果对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角晶格易轴XXZ反铁磁体的超固态序（特别是Y型序）在近期数值和实验中被讨论，但其微观起源、Y/Y'态选择机制及集体激发性质尚需理论澄清。

Method: 在π-通量狄拉克自旋液体上构建费米子部分子平均场理论，由在位自旋子相互作用项与最近邻各向异性自旋耦合产生平均场序；用无规相近似（RPA）计算集体模式；并施加两类外场（按磁矩取向的局域场、沿易轴的均匀场）以稳定Y态，同时调节自旋子相互作用强度与自旋耦合各向异性。

Result: 在有限参数区获得Y态自洽解及其竞争相Y'态（序平面内旋转90°的Y态）。平均场能量偏好Y'，尽管经典极限下两者严格简并；计入集体模式涨落的高阶修正后Y态可能量更低。RPA显示稳定化Y态的激发谱在M点出现软模（roton极小），并在无能隙戈德斯通模之上存在有能隙的赝戈德斯通模，后者已被实验观测到。

Conclusion: 部分子平均场+RPA框架可自洽描述三角晶格易轴XXZ模型中的Y型超固态序及其激发谱，解释了Y/Y'选择、赝戈德斯通模与roton软模，并与近期数值及实验结果相符。

Abstract: We study the supersolid order of the spin-1/2 easy-axis XXZ antiferromagnet on the triangular lattice using a fermionic parton mean-field theory built on the $π$-flux Dirac spin liquid, with the mean-field order generated by an on-site spinon interaction term and nearest-neighbor (anisotropic) spin couplings. The collective modes are obtained using the random-phase approximation (RPA). In a finite parameter region, we find the Y state as a self-consistent solution, together with the competing Y' state, which is a $90^\circ$-rotated Y state inside the order plane. The mean-field energy favors Y', even though the two states are exactly degenerate in the classical limit. However, the Y state can become lower in energy when higher-order corrections due to the fluctuations of the collective modes are considered. The Y state can also be stabilized on the mean-field level by applying small external fields, which we do in two different ways: moment-aligned local field and uniform field along the easy axis. In both cases, the RPA calculation for the stabilized Y state gives a roton minimum at the $M$ point in the excitation spectrum. A gapped pseudo-Goldstone mode above the gapless Goldstone mode is obtained as well, which has also been observed experimentally. Furthermore, we investigate the effects of tuning the spinon interaction strength and the anisotropy in spin couplings in our phenomenological model. Comparison with the recent numerical and experimental results is also discussed.

</details>


### [122] [Multimagnon bound states in the polarized phase of anisotropic antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08349)
*A. El Mendili,M. E. Zhitomirsky*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究单轴反铁磁体高场极化相中的单离子束缚磁振子态：精确求解两粒子问题得到束缚磁振子对色散，用Gershgorin圆定理给出结合能严格界；发现各向异性/交换比超过阈值时零总动量双磁振子束缚态成为低能激发，凝聚驱动向自旋向列相转变，并推广至n磁振子束缚态与场诱导多极相层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轴反铁磁体极化相中磁振子的束缚与凝聚机制及其与自旋向列相、多极相的联系尚需系统理论刻画，尤其需要严格的束缚能判据与超越两磁振子的推广。

Method: 在高场极化相中精确求解两粒子（双磁振子）问题，计算束缚对在连续谱上下方的色散；利用Gershgorin圆定理推导结合能的严格上下界；并发展大S易轴反铁磁体的强耦合方法，描述n≤2S的单离子n磁振子束缚态。

Result: 自旋1易轴系统中，当单离子各向异性与交换之比超过阈值时出现零总动量的低能双磁振子束缚态；随场降低束缚对凝聚并驱动进入自旋向列相。大S情形下多磁振子束缚态的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚给出由单离子各向异性稳定的场诱导多极相层级；与Na2BaNi(PO4)2和Sr2CoGe2O7实验对比显示定量吻合。

Conclusion: 单离子各向异性是稳定磁振子束缚与场诱导多极（含自旋向列）相的关键机制，所提强耦合框架在数量上与实验一致，为识别此类相变提供判据。

Abstract: We investigate single-ion bound magnon states in the high-field polarized phase of uniaxial antiferromagnets. By solving the two-particle problem exactly, we determine the dispersion of bound magnon pairs both below and above the continuum. We also obtain rigorous bounds on their binding energies by using the Gershgorin circle theorem. For spin-1 easy-axis systems, two-magnon bound states with zero total momentum emerge as low-energy excitations once the ratio of single-ion anisotropy to exchange exceeds a threshold value. As the field decreases, magnon pairs condense, driving a transition into a spin-nematic phase. Beyond the two-magnon problem, we develop a general strong-coupling approach that describes $n$-magnon single-ion bound states ($n\leq 2S$) in large-$S$ easy-axis antiferromagnets. The Bose-Einstein condensation of these multi-magnon excitations yields a rich hierarchy of field-induced multipolar phases stabilized by single-ion anisotropy. Comparisons with recent experiments on Na$_2$BaNi(PO$_4$)$_2$ and Sr$_2$CoGe$_2$O$_7$ illustrate the quantitative power of our theory.

</details>


### [123] [Direct transition from a fractional Chern insulator to a topological superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08475)
*Taige Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在ν=1/3排斥性Chern能带中,数值发现随带宽增大,Laughlin分数陈绝缘体(FCI)可直接连续转变为c₋=3/2拓扑超导体(TSC);作者建立广义涡旋增殖理论解释该转变,预言掺杂下出现超导穹顶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该TSC不源于弱耦合费米面不稳定性,而可能具有任意子起源,其与FCI之间的相变机制缺乏理论描述。核心问题是:FCI如何在大带宽下不经中间相直接连续转变为主体为TSC,以及临界点电子关联长度在偏离参考费米面动量处发散的物理机制。

Method: (1) 数值计算:在填充ν=1/3的排斥性相互作用Chern能带中扫描带宽,识别FCI到c₋=3/2 TSC的连续相变及临界谱;(2) 理论:将超导涡旋增殖理论推广到该情形——磁平移对称性把涡旋组织为六个谷,保持平移的涡旋增殖需引入附加规范场;(3) 用该理论计算临界谱的动量结构、电子关联长度的发散,并外推至偏离公度填充的掺杂情形。

Result: 数值证据支持FCI→TSC直接连续相变;临界点电子关联长度在偏离参考费米面的动量处发散;广义涡旋增殖理论复现了临界谱的动量结构并给出电子关联的发散;掺杂偏离公度填充时理论预言超导穹顶。

Conclusion: FCI与c₋=3/2 TSC之间存在由带宽驱动的直接连续量子相变,可由含六谷涡旋与附加规范场的增殖理论统一描述;该结果与拓扑超导的任意子起源相符,而不依赖于弱耦合费米面不稳定性。

Abstract: We find numerical evidence for a direct continuous transition from a Laughlin fractional Chern insulator (FCI) to a $c_-=3/2$ topological superconductor (TSC), driven by increasing bandwidth at filling $ν=1/3$ in a repulsively interacting Chern band. At the critical point, the electron correlation length diverges at momentum away from the reference Fermi surface. We develop a generalized theory of superconducting-vortex proliferation for this transition. Magnetic translation organizes the vortices into six valleys, so translation-preserving proliferation requires additional gauge fields. The resulting theory reproduces the momentum structure of the critical spectrum and captures the divergent electron correlation. Upon doping away from commensurate filling, the theory gives rise to a superconducting dome. These results are consistent with an anyonic origin of the topological superconductivity in the absence of a weak-coupling Fermi-surface instability.

</details>
